    private static function hub1_posts_v150223($ctx, $page_title, $key = 'generic') {
        $cat_ids = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_map('intval', (array)($ctx['hub_post_cat_ids'] ?? ($ctx['cat_ids'] ?? []))))));
        $posts = self::top_posts_by_importance($cat_ids, 3);
        $copy = self::hub1_section_copy_v150358($key, $page_title);
        $count = 0;
        $cards = '';
        foreach ((array)$posts as $post) {
            if (!$post instanceof WP_Post) { continue; }
            $url = get_permalink($post);
            $image = (string)get_the_post_thumbnail_url($post->ID, 'large');
            $cats = get_the_category($post->ID);
            $label = !empty($cats) && $cats[0] instanceof WP_Term ? $cats[0]->name : $page_title;
            $excerpt = wp_trim_words(self::clean_excerpt($post), 14, '…');
            $cards .= '<article class="pa223-guide">';
            if ($image !== '') {
                $cards .= '<a class="pa223-guide-image" href="' . esc_url($url) . '"><img src="' . esc_url($image) . '" alt="' . esc_attr(get_the_title($post)) . '" loading="lazy"></a>';
            } else {
                $cards .= '<a class="pa223-guide-image pa223-fallback" href="' . esc_url($url) . '">' . self::hub1_icon_v150227('wissen') . '</a>';
            }
            $cards .= '<div class="pa223-guide-copy"><span>' . esc_html($label) . '</span><h3><a href="' . esc_url($url) . '">' . esc_html(get_the_title($post)) . '</a></h3>'
                . ($excerpt !== '' ? '<p>' . esc_html($excerpt) . '</p>' : '<p aria-hidden="true"></p>')
                . '<a class="pa223-button pa223-read" href="' . esc_url($url) . '"><span>Weiterlesen</span></a></div></article>';
            $count++;
        }

        $out = '<section class="pa223-section pa223-guides"><header class="pa356-section-heading"><span>Ratgeber zu ' . esc_html($page_title) . '</span><h2>' . esc_html($copy['posts']) . '</h2></header>';
        if ($count > 0) {
            $out .= '<div class="pa223-guide-grid count-' . intval($count) . '" data-pftk-post-card-count="' . intval($count) . '">' . $cards . '</div>';
        }
        $out .= self::bridge_v150363('hub1', $page_title, $key);
        return $out . '</section>';
    }

    private static function hub1_bottom_editorial_v150418($key) {
        static $catalog = null;
        if (!is_array($catalog)) {
            $catalog = [];
            $path = plugin_dir_path(__FILE__) . 'assets/hub1-bottom-editorial-v150418.json';
            if (is_readable($path)) {
                $decoded = json_decode((string)file_get_contents($path), true);
                if (is_array($decoded) && (string)($decoded['contract'] ?? '') === 'PFERDE_ATELIER_HUB1_BOTTOM_EDITORIAL_V150418' && isset($decoded['pages']) && is_array($decoded['pages'])) {
                    $catalog = $decoded['pages'];
                }
            }
        }
        $key = sanitize_key((string)$key);
        if ($key === '' || empty($catalog[$key]) || !is_array($catalog[$key])) { return ''; }
        $entry = $catalog[$key];
        $title = trim((string)($entry['title'] ?? ''));
        $paragraphs = isset($entry['paragraphs']) && is_array($entry['paragraphs']) ? $entry['paragraphs'] : [];
        if ($title === '' || empty($paragraphs)) { return ''; }
        $links = [
            'Ausrüstung' => home_url('/ausruestung/'),
            'Stall' => home_url('/stall/'),

            'Gesundheit' => home_url('/gesundheit/'),
            'Training' => home_url('/training/'),
            'Transport' => home_url('/transport/'),
            'Wissen' => home_url('/wissen/'),
        ];
        $rendered = [];
        foreach ($paragraphs as $paragraph) {
            $paragraph = trim((string)$paragraph);
            if ($paragraph === '') { continue; }
            $html = esc_html($paragraph);
            foreach ($links as $name => $url) {
                $token = esc_html('[[' . $name . ']]');
                if (strpos($html, $token) === false) { continue; }
                $html = str_replace($token, '<a href="' . esc_url($url) . '">' . esc_html($name) . '</a>', $html);
            }
            $rendered[] = $html;
        }
        if (empty($rendered)) { return ''; }
        $lead = '<p class="pa419-hub1-bottom-lead">' . array_shift($rendered) . '</p>';
        $block_headings = isset($entry['block_headings']) && is_array($entry['block_headings']) ? array_values($entry['block_headings']) : [];

        $copy = '';

            $h1 = trim((string)($block_headings[0] ?? ''));
            $h2 = trim((string)($block_headings[1] ?? ''));

                . '<div class="pa437-hub1-bottom-body is-right"><p>' . $rendered[1] . '</p></div>';
        } else {

                    . ($block_heading !== '' ? '<h4>' . esc_html($block_heading) . '</h4>' : '')
                    . '<p>' . $html . '</p></article>';
            }
        }
        $css = '<style id="pftk-hub1-bottom-editorial-v150419-css">'
            . '.pa419-hub1-bottom{margin:64px 0 0!important;padding:clamp(42px,5.5vw,70px) clamp(22px,5vw,64px)!important;border:1px solid rgba(53,66,42,.18)!important;border-top:0!important;background:#FBF8F2!important;color:#35422A!important}'

    /**
     * V1.50.333 – stabile Affiliate-Schnittstelle für den tatsächlich live
     * verwendeten Leaf-SEITEN-Renderer. Keine Platzhalterausgabe.
     */
    private static function leaf_page_affiliate_slot_v150333($page_id, $slot) {
        $page_id = absint($page_id);
        $slot = sanitize_key((string)$slot);
        if ($page_id <= 0 || $slot === '') { return ''; }
        $context = [
            'contract' => self::AFFILIATE_CONTRACT,
            'design_version' => self::VERSION,
            'template' => 'leaf_page',
        ];
        $candidate = apply_filters('pftk_leaf_page_affiliate_slot_html', '', $page_id, $slot, $context);
        if (trim((string)$candidate) === '' && function_exists('shortcode_exists') && shortcode_exists('pp_affiliate_slot')) {
            $candidate = do_shortcode('[pp_affiliate_slot type="' . esc_attr($slot) . '" intent="portal_context"]');
        }
        return trim((string)$candidate);
    }

    private static function leaf_page_real_banner_v150333($page_id) {
        $candidate = self::leaf_page_affiliate_slot_v150333($page_id, 'product_after_category_tiles');
        return self::category_affiliate_banner_is_real_v150308($candidate) ? $candidate : '';
    }

    private static function leaf_page_real_products_v150333($page_id) {
        $products = [];
        for ($index = 1; $index <= 3; $index++) {
            $slot = 'category_product_' . $index;
            $candidate = self::leaf_page_affiliate_slot_v150333($page_id, $slot);
            if (!self::category_affiliate_product_is_real_v150308($candidate)) { continue; }
            $products[] = '<div class="pa266-product is-real" data-pa266-slot="' . esc_attr($slot) . '">' . $candidate . '</div>';
        }
        return array_slice($products, 0, 3);
    }

    private static function render_leaf_level_v150272() {
        $ctx = self::context('category');
        $page_id = absint(get_queried_object_id());
        if ($page_id <= 0) { $page_id = absint(get_the_ID()); }
        if ($page_id > 0) { self::$category_intro_rendered[$page_id] = true; self::$page_shortline_rendered[$page_id] = true; }
        $title = trim((string)get_the_title($page_id));
        if ($title === '') { $title = 'Themenbereich'; }
        $texts = self::leaf_level_text_profile_v150272($page_id, $title);
        $posts = self::leaf_level_posts_v150272($ctx, 12);
        $post_count = count((array)$posts);
        $affiliate_banner = self::leaf_page_real_banner_v150333($page_id);
        $affiliate_products = self::leaf_page_real_products_v150333($page_id);
        $affiliate_product_count = count($affiliate_products);
        $page_image = self::portal_page_image_block_v150383($page_id, 'leaf');

        $out = '<div class="pa272-leaf pa333-leaf-page" data-pa333-leaf-page="1">';
        $out .= $page_image;
        $out .= '<section class="pa272-intro"><span>' . esc_html($texts['kicker']) . '</span><h1>' . esc_html($title) . '</h1>' . self::deck_intro_markup_v150390($texts['intro']) . '</section>';
        $out .= '<style>.page-id-' . intval($page_id) . ' .entry-header,.page-id-' . intval($page_id) . ' .ast-single-entry-banner,.page-id-' . intval($page_id) . ' h1.entry-title,.page-id-' . intval($page_id) . ' .wp-block-post-title{display:none!important}</style>';

        // Partnerbereich ausschließlich über den Beiträgen und nur bei realer Befüllung.

            $out .= '<section class="pa272-section pa333-partner-section"><header><span>' . esc_html($texts['banner_kicker']) . '</span><h2>' . esc_html($texts['banner_title']) . '</h2></header><div class="pa272-banner-slot is-real">' . $affiliate_banner . '</div></section>';

        if ($post_count > 0) {

            foreach ((array)$posts as $post) {
                if ($post instanceof WP_Post) { $out .= self::render_leaf_post_card_v150272($post); }

            $out .= '</div>';
        } else {
            $out .= '<p class="pa272-empty">Zu ' . esc_html($title) . ' sind noch keine Einzelbeiträge veröffentlicht.</p>';

        $out .= '<style>.page-id-' . intval($page_id) . ' .entry-header,.page-id-' . intval($page_id) . ' .ast-single-entry-banner,.page-id-' . intval($page_id) . ' h1.entry-title,.page-id-' . intval($page_id) . ' .wp-block-post-title{display:none!important}</style>';
        // V1.50.307 – kompakte Karten ohne redundanten Kurztext.
        // Sichtbar bleiben ausschließlich Icon, dunkelolive Überschrift und CTA.
        $out .= '<section class="pa266-section pa299-types-section"><header><span>Überblick</span><h2>' . esc_html($texts['types_title']) . '</h2></header><div class="pa266-type-grid count-' . count($visible) . '">';
        foreach ($visible as $item) {
            $item_title = trim((string)($item->title ?? ''));
            $icon = self::category_utility_icon_v150304($item_title);
            $card_text = self::category_type_card_text_v150268($item_title, $title);
            // V1.50.309 – ein einziges Linkziel pro Kachel: Die komplette
            // Beitragsart-Kachel ist klickbar. Der sichtbare CTA ist kein
            // verschachtelter Link, sondern ein rein visueller Bestandteil.
            $out .= '<a class="pa266-type pa309-type-link" aria-label="' . esc_attr($card_text['title'] . ' entdecken') . '" href="' . esc_url($item->url ?? '#') . '">' . $icon . '<h3>' . esc_html($card_text['title']) . '</h3><span class="pa266-type-button" aria-hidden="true">Entdecken</span></a>';
        }
        if (count($items) > 6) {
$out .= '<a class="pa266-type pa266-all pa309-type-link" aria-label="Alle Inhalte entdecken" href="#pa266-posts">' . self::category_utility_icon_v150304('Alle Inhalte') . '<h3>Alle Inhalte</h3><span class="pa266-type-button" aria-hidden="true">Entdecken</span></a>';
        }
        if (empty($visible)) { $out .= '<p class="pa266-empty">Für ' . esc_html($title) . ' sind noch keine Beitragsarten veröffentlicht.</p>'; }
        $out .= '</div></section><span id="pa266-all-content" class="pa297-anchor" aria-hidden="true"></span>';

        // V1.50.341 – verbindliche Reihenfolge auf der gesamten Ebene
        // derselben strukturellen Tiefe wie „Regendecken“:
        // Kategorieverlinkungen -> Banner -> Beitragsübersicht -> Produkte ->
        // Direktwerbeplatz ausschließlich als Fallback. Gestaltung, Texte,
        // Inhalte, Karten und Werbemittel bleiben unverändert.
        $affiliate_layout = self::category_affiliate_layout_v150341($title);
        $affiliate_banner = (string)($affiliate_layout['banner'] ?? '');
        $affiliate_products = (array)($affiliate_layout['products'] ?? []);
        $affiliate_product_count = count($affiliate_products);

        if ($affiliate_banner !== '') {
            $out .= '<section class="pa266-section pa266-network"><header><span>' . esc_html($texts['network_kicker']) . '</span><h2>' . esc_html($texts['network_title']) . '</h2></header><div class="pa266-network-slot is-real">' . $affiliate_banner . '</div></section>';
        }

        $popular_source_ids = !empty($ctx['hub_post_cat_ids']) ? (array)$ctx['hub_post_cat_ids'] : (array)($ctx['cat_ids'] ?? []);
        $popular_cat_ids = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_map('intval', $popular_source_ids))));
        $popular_posts = self::top_posts_by_importance($popular_cat_ids, 3);
        if (!empty($popular_posts)) {
            $out .= '<section class="pa266-section pa297-popular"><header><span>' . esc_html($texts['popular_kicker']) . '</span><h2>' . esc_html($texts['popular_title']) . '</h2>' . (trim((string)$texts['popular_intro']) !== '' ? '<p>' . esc_html($texts['popular_intro']) . '</p>' : '') . '</header><div class="pa297-popular-grid count-' . intval(count((array)$popular_posts)) . '" data-pftk-post-card-count="' . intval(count((array)$popular_posts)) . '">';
            foreach ((array)$popular_posts as $post) {
                if ($post instanceof WP_Post) { $out .= self::render_leaf_post_card_v150272($post); }
            }
            $out .= '</div></section>';
        }

        if ($affiliate_product_count > 0) {
            $out .= '<section class="pa266-section pa266-products"><header><span>' . esc_html($texts['products_kicker']) . '</span><h2>' . esc_html($texts['products_title']) . '</h2></header><div class="pa266-product-grid count-' . intval($affiliate_product_count) . '" data-pa266-product-count="' . intval($affiliate_product_count) . '">' . implode('', $affiliate_products) . '</div></section>';
        }
        $out .= '<section class="pa266-section pa266-direct pftk-direct-responsive-v150360"><header><span>' . esc_html($texts['direct_kicker']) . '</span><h2>' . esc_html($texts['direct_title']) . '</h2>' . (trim((string)$texts['direct_intro']) !== '' ? '<p>' . esc_html($texts['direct_intro']) . '</p>' : '') . '</header>' . self::start_ad_slot_v150162('category-direct-' . $page_id, 'category', 'wide') . '</section>';
        // V1.50.305: Der generische Kaufberatungs-/Entscheidungsblock ist auf
        // dieser Ebene redundant zu Beitragsarten, meistgelesenen Beiträgen
        // und Empfehlungen und wird deshalb nicht mehr ausgegeben.
        $out .= '</div>';
        $out .= '<style id="pa266-category-css">.pa266-category{--o:#35422A;--a:#C89214;--s:#F2E8D6;--g:#4F5650;max-width:1320px;margin:0 auto;padding:12px 24px 72px;color:var(--g)}.pa266-category      * Kompatibilitätsmethoden für bestehende interne Tests und Aufrufer.

    private static function hub2_affiliate_banner_v150320() {
        $layout = self::hub2_affiliate_layout_v150339('');
        return (string)($layout['banner'] ?? '');
    }

    private static function hub2_affiliate_products_v150320($topic = '') {
        $layout = self::hub2_affiliate_layout_v150339($topic);
        return (array)($layout['products'] ?? []);
    }

    private static function hub2_placeholder_banner_v150263($kind, $topic, $texts = []) {
        $is_direct = $kind === 'direct';
        $label = $is_direct ? 'Direktwerbung' : (string)($texts['network_banner_label'] ?? 'Affiliate-Partner');
        $title = $is_direct ? 'Hier könnte Ihre Werbung stehen' : (string)($texts['network_banner_title'] ?? ('Partnerangebot rund um ' . $topic));
        $text = $is_direct
            ? 'Exklusive Werbefläche im passenden Themenumfeld des Pferde Ateliers.'
            : (string)($texts['network_banner_text'] ?? 'Ein thematisch passendes Angebot aus einem Affiliate-Netzwerk.');
        $cta = $is_direct ? 'Werbeplatz anfragen' : (string)($texts['network_button'] ?? 'Angebot ansehen');
        $url = $is_direct ? home_url('/kontakt/') : '#';
        return '<a class="pa255-banner ' . ($is_direct ? 'is-direct' : 'is-network') . '" href="' . esc_url($url) . '"><div class="pa255-banner-meta"><span>' . esc_html($label) . '</span></div><div class="pa255-banner-copy"><strong>' . esc_html($title) . '</strong><p>' . esc_html($text) . '</p></div><div class="pa255-banner-action"><b>' . esc_html($cta) . '</b></div></a>';
    }

    private static function hub2_product_slot_v150263($index, $topic, $texts) {
        $index = max(1, min(3, intval($index)));
        $slot = 'hub2_product_' . $index;
        if (function_exists('shortcode_exists') && shortcode_exists('pp_affiliate_slot')) {
            $candidate = (string)do_shortcode('[pp_affiliate_slot type="' . esc_attr($slot) . '" intent="portal_context"]');
            if (self::hub1_product_content_is_real_v150214($candidate)) {
                return '<div class="pa255-product is-real" data-pa255-slot="' . esc_attr($slot) . '">' . $candidate . '</div>';
            }
        }
        $title = (string)($texts['product_' . $index . '_title'] ?? 'Passende Produktempfehlung');
        $text = (string)($texts['product_' . $index . '_text'] ?? ('Eine passende Empfehlung rund um ' . $topic . '.'));
        $label = (string)($texts['product_label'] ?? 'Produktempfehlung');
        $button = (string)($texts['product_button'] ?? 'Produkt ansehen');
        return '<article class="pa255-product is-placeholder" data-pa255-slot="' . esc_attr($slot) . '"><div class="pa255-product-image"><span>Produktbild</span><small>aus Affiliate-Feed</small></div><div><span>' . esc_html($label) . '</span><h3>' . esc_html($title) . '</h3><p>' . esc_html($text) . '</p><b>' . esc_html($button) . '</b></div></article>';
    }

    /**
     * V1.50.326 – kurze Vorschautexte ausschließlich im letzten Hub-2-
     * Ratgeberblock. Der redaktionelle Beitrag bleibt unverändert; gekürzt
     * wird nur die Kartenansicht. Maximal 12 Wörter, keine Textwand.
     */
    private static function hub2_post_excerpt_v150326($post) {
        $excerpt = trim((string)self::clean_excerpt($post));
        if ($excerpt === '') { return ''; }
        return wp_trim_words($excerpt, 12, ' …');
    }

    private static function hub2_posts_v150263($ctx, $topic, $texts) {
        $cat_ids = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_map('intval', (array)($ctx['hub_post_cat_ids'] ?? ($ctx['cat_ids'] ?? []))))));
        $posts = self::top_posts_by_importance($cat_ids, 3);
        $out = '<section class="pa255-section pa255-guides" id="pa255-guides"><header><span>' . esc_html($texts['posts_kicker']) . '</span><h2>' . esc_html($texts['posts_title']) . '</h2>' . (!empty($texts['posts_intro']) ? '<p>' . esc_html($texts['posts_intro']) . '</p>' : '') . '</header><div class="pa255-guide-grid count-3" data-pftk-post-card-count="3">';
        $rendered = 0;
        foreach ((array)$posts as $post) {
            if (!$post instanceof WP_Post) { continue; }
            $image = (string)get_the_post_thumbnail_url($post->ID, 'large');
            $excerpt = self::hub2_post_excerpt_v150326($post);
            $out .= '<article class="pa255-guide">';
            if ($image !== '') {
                $out .= '<a class="pa255-guide-image" href="' . esc_url(get_permalink($post)) . '"><img src="' . esc_url($image) . '" alt="' . esc_attr(get_the_title($post)) . '" loading="lazy"></a>';
            } else {
                $out .= '<a class="pa255-guide-image is-empty" href="' . esc_url(get_permalink($post)) . '"><span>Beitragsbild</span></a>';
            }
            $out .= '<div><span>' . esc_html($texts['post_label']) . '</span><h3><a href="' . esc_url(get_permalink($post)) . '">' . esc_html(get_the_title($post)) . '</a></h3>' . ($excerpt !== '' ? '<p>' . esc_html($excerpt) . '</p>' : '') . '<a class="pa255-textlink" href="' . esc_url(get_permalink($post)) . '">' . esc_html($texts['post_button']) . '</a></div></article>';
            $rendered++;
        }
        while ($rendered < 3) {
            $out .= '<article class="pa255-guide is-placeholder"><div class="pa255-guide-image is-empty"><span>Beitragsbild</span></div><div><span>' . esc_html($texts['post_label']) . '</span><h3>' . esc_html($texts['post_placeholder_title']) . '</h3><p>' . esc_html($texts['post_placeholder_text']) . '</p><a class="pa255-textlink" href="' . esc_url(home_url('/journal/')) . '">' . esc_html($texts['post_placeholder_button']) . '</a></div></article>';
            $rendered++;
        }
        return $out . '</div></section>';
    }

    private static function render_hub2_v150255() {
        $ctx = self::context('hub2');
        $page_id = !is_admin() && is_singular('page') ? absint(get_queried_object_id()) : 0;
        if ($page_id <= 0 && !is_admin()) { $page_id = absint(get_the_ID()); }
        $page_title = $page_id > 0 ? trim((string)get_the_title($page_id)) : trim((string)self::text('hub2', 'hero_title'));
        if ($page_title === '') { $page_title = 'Themenbereich'; }
        $topic = self::hub2_topic_label_v150255($page_title);
        $kicker = self::hub2_kicker_v150264($page_id, $page_title);
        $intro_text = self::hub2_intro_v150264($page_id, $page_title);
        $texts = self::hub2_text_profile_v150269($page_id, $page_title, $topic);

        $items = self::published_page_items($ctx['top_items'] ?? [], 0, false);
        $items = self::sort_structural_items($items);
        $primary_items = array_slice($items, 0, 6);
        $remaining = array_slice($items, 6);

        $hero_item = (object)['title' => $page_title, 'url' => $page_id > 0 ? get_permalink($page_id) : '#'];
        $hero_key = self::portal_topic_icon_key_v150251($hero_item);
        if ($hero_key === '') { $hero_key = self::portal_main_icon_key_v150251($hero_item); }
        $hero_icon = self::portal_icon_markup_v150251($hero_key, 'hub1');
        $page_image = self::portal_page_image_block_v150383($page_id, 'hub2');
        $out = '<div class="pa255-hub2" data-pa255-isolated-hub2="1">' . self::hub2_css_v150255();
        $out .= $page_image;
        $out .= '<section class="pa255-intro"><div class="pa255-crown-icon">' . $hero_icon . '</div><span>' . esc_html($kicker) . '</span><h1>' . esc_html($page_title) . '</h1>' . self::deck_intro_markup_v150390($intro_text) . '</section>';
        $out .= '<script>(function(){var t=' . wp_json_encode($page_title) . ';function n(s){return (s||"").replace(/\s+/g," " ).trim().toLowerCase()}function h(){document.querySelectorAll(".entry-header,.ast-single-entry-banner,.page-header,.wp-block-post-title,h1.entry-title,.page-title").forEach(function(e){if(e.closest(".pa255-hub2"))return;var x=n(e.textContent);if(x===n(t)||e.querySelector("h1.entry-title,.wp-block-post-title,.page-title")){e.style.setProperty("display","none","important")}})}if(document.readyState==="loading")document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",h,{once:true});else h()})();</script>';

        $out .= '<section class="pa255-section pa255-topics" id="pa255-topics"><header><span>' . esc_html($texts['topics_kicker']) . '</span><h2>' . esc_html($texts['topics_title']) . '</h2></header><div class="pa255-topic-grid count-' . count($primary_items) . '">';
        $seen_topic_teasers = [];
        foreach ($primary_items as $item) {
            // V1.50.374: Jede sichtbare Unterkategorie erhaelt ihr fest
            // zugeordnetes, sluggebundenes Motiv. Das Elternicon darf innerhalb
            // der Kachelschleife nicht mehr wiederholt werden.
            $icon = self::portal_structural_icon_markup_v150374($item);
            $teaser = self::hub2_tile_teaser_v150318($item, $page_title, $seen_topic_teasers);
            $teaser_signature = self::normalize_match_key($teaser);
            if ($teaser_signature !== '') { $seen_topic_teasers[$teaser_signature] = true; }
            $out .= '<a class="pa255-topic" href="' . esc_url($item->url) . '">' . $icon . '<h3>' . esc_html($item->title) . '</h3>' . ($teaser !== '' ? '<p>' . esc_html($teaser) . '</p>' : '') . '<b>' . esc_html($texts['topic_button']) . '</b></a>';
        }
        if (empty($primary_items)) { $out .= '<div class="pa255-empty">' . esc_html($texts['topics_empty']) . '</div>'; }
        $out .= '</div></section>';

        // V1.50.339 – verbindliche Reihenfolge der Referenz „Decken“ für die
        // gesamte Hub-2-Ebene: Unterkategorien, Partnerbanner, redaktionelle
        // Beitragsvorschau, Produktvorschläge, danach nur bei Bedarf der
        // direkt buchbare Fallback-Werbeplatz.
        $affiliate_layout = self::hub2_affiliate_layout_v150339($topic);
        $affiliate_banner = (string)($affiliate_layout['banner'] ?? '');
        $affiliate_products = (array)($affiliate_layout['products'] ?? []);
        $affiliate_product_count = count($affiliate_products);

        if ($affiliate_banner !== '') {
            $out .= '<section class="pa255-section pa255-network"><header><span>' . esc_html($texts['network_kicker']) . '</span><h2>' . esc_html($texts['network_title']) . '</h2></header><div class="pa255-network-slot is-real">' . $affiliate_banner . '</div></section>';
        }

        // Der redaktionelle Block trennt Partnerbanner und die unteren
        // Werbezonen sichtbar voneinander.
        $out .= self::hub2_posts_v150263($ctx, $topic, $texts);
       \n\n========== EXACT HIT ==========\n\nPATTERN=pftk-post\nOFFSET=967843\nat(2,minmax(0,1fr))}.pa255-product-grid.count-1{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,360px)}.pa255-product-grid.count-2,.pa255-product-grid.count-3{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,360px));justify-content:center}.pa255-banner{grid-template-columns:1fr}.pa255-banner-action{justify-content:flex-start}.pa255-banner b{justify-self:start}}'
            .             . '</style>';

    private static function render_design_affiliate_slot($type, $slot) {
        $slot = sanitize_key($slot);
        if ($slot === '') { return ''; }
        // Startseiten- und Ebene-3-Werbeplätze werden vollständig in der
        // Affiliate-Zentrale gesteuert. Das Designplugin stellt nur die Fläche.
        $centrally_managed = in_array($slot, ['start_after_topics', 'product_after_category_tiles'], true);
        if (!$centrally_managed && !self::visible($type, 'show_slot_' . $slot)) { return ''; }
        if (!function_exists('shortcode_exists') || !shortcode_exists('pp_affiliate_slot')) {
            if ($slot === 'product_after_category_tiles') { return ''; }
            return self::affiliate_slot_visual_placeholder($slot);
        }

        $intent = in_array($slot, ['start_after_topics', 'hub_top_cta', 'hub_after_cards', 'category_recommendation', 'product_after_category_tiles'], true) ? 'portal_context' : 'fallback';
        $out = do_shortcode('[pp_affiliate_slot type="' . esc_attr($slot) . '" intent="' . esc_attr($intent) . '"]');
        if (trim(wp_strip_all_tags((string)$out)) === '' && strpos((string)$out, '<img') === false && strpos((string)$out, '<a') === false) {
            if ($slot === 'product_after_category_tiles') { return ''; }
            return self::affiliate_slot_visual_placeholder($slot);
        }
        return $out;
    }

    private static function affiliate_slot_admin_placeholder($slot, $reason) {
        return '<section class="pftk-section pftk-affiliate-slot-debug" data-pftk-slot="' . esc_attr($slot) . '"><div class="pftk-empty">Affiliate-Slot ' . esc_html($slot) . ' aktiv: ' . esc_html($reason) . '.</div></section>';
    }

    private static function affiliate_slot_visual_placeholder($slot) {
        return '<div class="pftk-affiliate-placeholder-v15072" data-pftk-affiliate-placeholder="' . esc_attr($slot) . '" role="img" aria-label="Affiliate-Werbefläche"><span>Affiliate-Werbefläche</span><small>' . esc_html($slot) . '</small></div>';
    }

    private static function render_hero($type) {
        $title = self::text($type, 'hero_title');
        $lead = self::text($type, 'hero_lead');
        $eyebrow = self::text($type, 'hero_eyebrow');
        if ($title === '' && $lead === '' && $eyebrow === '') { return ''; }

        $image = self::media($type, 'hero_image');
        if ($type === 'start' && $image === '' && is_singular('page')) {
            $fallback_image = get_the_post_thumbnail_url(absint(get_queried_object_id()), 'full');
            if (is_string($fallback_image)) { $image = $fallback_image; }
        }
        $visual = '';
        if ($image !== '') {
            $alt = self::media($type, 'hero_alt') ?: $title;
            $inner = '<img src="' . esc_url($image) . '" alt="' . esc_attr($alt) . '">';
            $visual = '<div class="pftk-hero-visual">' . self::wrap_link($inner, self::media($type, 'hero_url'), self::media($type, 'hero_target'), false, 'pftk-image-link') . '</div>';
        }

        $heading_tag = $type === 'start' ? 'h1' : 'h2';
        return '<section class="pftk-hero pftk-curve ' . ($visual ? 'has-visual' : 'no-visual') . '">' .
            '<div class="pftk-hero-copy">' .
            ($eyebrow !== '' ? '<p class="pftk-heading pftk-heading-hero_eyebrow">' . esc_html($eyebrow) . '</p>' : '') .
            ($title !== '' ? '<' . $heading_tag . ' class="pftk-heading pftk-heading-hero_title">' . esc_html($title) . '</' . $heading_tag . '>' : '') .
            ($lead !== '' ? '<p class="pftk-lead">' . esc_html($lead) . '</p>' : '') .
            '<div class="pftk-actions"><a href="#pftk-topics">' . esc_html(self::text($type, 'hero_button_1')) . '</a><a href="#pftk-posts">' . esc_html(self::text($type, 'hero_button_2')) . '</a></div>' .
            '</div>' . $visual . '</section>';
    }

    private static function render_seo($type, $pos) {
        $title = self::raw_text($type, 'seo_' . $pos . '_title');
        $body = self::raw_text($type, 'seo_' . $pos . '_body');
        if (trim($title) === '' && trim($body) === '') { return ''; }
        $key = 'seo_' . $pos . '_title';
        return '<section class="pftk-seo pftk-section pftk-curve">' .
            (trim($title) !== '' ? '<h2 class="pftk-heading pftk-heading-' . esc_attr($key) . '">' . esc_html($title) . '</h2>' : '') .
            (trim($body) !== '' ? '<div class="pftk-seo-text">' . wpautop(esc_html($body)) . '</div>' : '') .
            '</section>';
    }

    private static function render_topics($type, $ctx) {
        $force_all_category_tiles = !empty($ctx['force_all_category_tiles']);
        if (in_array($type, ['start', 'hub1', 'hub2'], true) && !$force_all_category_tiles) {
            $items = self::published_page_items($ctx['top_items'] ?? [], 0, $type === 'start');
            $items = self::sort_structural_items($items);
            if ($type === 'start') { $limit = 8; }
            elseif ($type === 'hub1') { $limit = 5; }
            else { $limit = 5; }
            $items = array_slice($items, 0, $limit);
        } else {
            $items = self::sort_items($ctx['top_items'] ?? []);
        }
        // Automatische Pflicht-Kategorie-Kacheln auf Ebene 3 bekommen keine
        // generische Überschrift/Intro. Der Nutzer sieht nur die relevanten Kacheln.
        $item_type = $force_all_category_tiles ? 'category' : 'page';
        return self::cards_section($type, 'pftk-topics', 'topics_title', 'topics_intro', $items, $item_type, $force_all_category_tiles);
    }

    private static function start_page_url_v150161($slugs, $fallback = '#') {
        foreach ((array)$slugs as $slug) {
            $page = get_page_by_path(sanitize_title($slug));
            if ($page instanceof WP_Post && $page->post_status === 'publish') { return get_permalink($page); }
        }
        return $fallback;
    }

    private static function start_icon_v150161($key) {
        return self::portal_icon_markup_v150251(self::portal_named_icon_key_v150251($key), 'start');
    }


    private static function start_category_description_v150161($title) {
        $key = self::normalize_match_key((string)$title);
        $map = [
            'ausruestung' => 'Ausrüstung, Pflege, Sattelzeug und Zubehör.',
            'fuetterung' => 'Futter, Ergänzung und praktische Fütterung.',
            'gesundheit' => 'Vorsorge, Pflege und Gesundheit verstehen.',
            'ausbildung' => 'Training, Reitweisen und gemeinsames Lernen.',
            'stall' => 'Stall, Weide, Haltung und tägliche Organisation.',
            'haltung' => 'Stall, Weide, Haltung und tägliche Organisation.',
            'pferd' => 'Verhalten, Alltag und das Pferd besser verstehen.',
            'ratgeber' => 'Orientierung, Vergleiche und sichere Entscheidungen.',
            'anzeigenmarkt' => 'Anbieter, Dienstleistungen und Angebote finden.',
            'journal' => 'Wissen, Geschichten und neue Blickwinkel.',
            'magazin' => 'Wissen, Geschichten und neue Blickwinkel.',
        ];
        foreach ($map as $token => $description) {
            if ($key === $token || strpos($key, $token . '-') === 0 || strpos($key, '-' . $token) !== false) { return $description; }
        }
        return 'Direkt zur nächsten Themenebene im Pferde Atelier.';
    }

    private static function start_categories_v150161() {
        $ctx = self::context('start');
        $items = self::valid_card_items($ctx['top_items'] ?? []);
        $items = array_slice($items, 0, 8);
        if (empty($items)) { return ''; }
        $out = '<section class="pftk-start-section-v150161" id="pftk-start-themen"><div class="pftk-start-heading-v150161"><span>Themenwelten</span><h2>Direkt zur passenden Kategorie</h2><p>Wähle deinen Bereich und gehe ohne Umwege zur nächsten Ebene.</p></div><div class="pftk-start-category-grid-v150161">';
        foreach ($items as $item) {
            if (!is_object($item)) { continue; }
            $title = trim((string)($item->title ?? ''));
            $url = trim((string)($item->url ?? '#'));
            if ($title === '') { continue; }
            $icon = self::portal_icon_markup_v150251(self::portal_main_icon_key_v150251($item), 'start');
            $out .= '<a class="pftk-start-category-card-v150161" href="' . esc_url($url) . '">' . $icon . '<h3>' . esc_html($title) . '</h3><p>' . esc_html(self::start_category_description_v150161($title)) . '</p><b>Weiter zur Kategorie <span>›</span></b></a>';
        }
        return $out . '</div></section>';
    }

    private static function start_ad_slot_v150162($slot_id, $placement, $format = 'wide') {
        $base = plugin_dir_url(__FILE__) . 'assets/';
        $wide = $base . 'werbeplatzhalter-startseite-banner-v150162.webp?ver=' . rawurlencode(self::VERSION);
        $wide_clean = $base . 'werbeplatzhalter-startseite-banner-clean-v150189.webp?ver=' . rawurlencode(self::VERSION);
        $card = $base . 'werbeplatzhalter-startseite-karte-v150162.webp?ver=' . rawurlencode(self::VERSION);
        $portrait = $base . 'werbeplatzhalter-startseite-hochformat-v150162.webp?ver=' . rawurlencode(self::VERSION);
        $is_card = ($format === 'card');
        if ($format === 'wide') {
            $slot_key = (string)$slot_id;
            $is_responsive_direct = in_array($slot_key, ['home-wide-01','hub1-direct-01'], true)
                || strpos($slot_key, 'hub2-direct-') === 0
                || strpos($slot_key, 'category-direct-') === 0
                || strpos($slot_key, 'leaf-page-direct-') === 0
                || strpos($slot_key, 'leaf-category-direct-') === 0
                || strpos($slot_key, 'journal-direct-') === 0;
            if ($is_responsive_direct) {
                // V1.50.354: Ein einziger isolierter Renderer fuer die Startseiten-
                // Werbeflaeche. Dasselbe Pferdemotiv bleibt bei jeder Breite vollstaendig
                // erhalten; Logo, Titel, Unterzeile und CTA werden wieder in der
                // bestaetigten Originalgroesse gerendert. Keine Altklasse dieses
                // Banners wird verwendet, damit fruehere Media-Query-Kaskaden hier
                // technisch nicht mehr eingreifen koennen.
                $logo = $base . 'pferde-a\n\n========== EXACT HIT ==========\n\nPATTERN=pftk-post\nOFFSET=1016828\n63" href="' . esc_url($market_url) . '">Anbieter &amp; Angebote</a>'
            . '</div>'
            . '</div></section>';
    }

    private static function start_subcategories_v150170() {
        $definitions = [
            ['Regendecken', 'Schutz bei Regen und wechselhaftem Wetter', ['ausruestung/decken/regendecken', 'pferdedecken-regendecken', 'regendecken'], 'equipment'],

        foreach ($cards as $card) {
            $out .= '<a href="'.esc_url($card[2]).'">'.self::start_art_v150170($card[3]).'<span><strong>'.esc_html($card[0]).'</strong><small>'.esc_html($card[1]).'</small></span><b>›</b></a>';
        }
        return $out.'</div></section>';
    }

    private static function start_posts_v150170() {
        $candidates = get_posts([
            'post_type' => 'post', 'post_status' => 'publish', 'posts_per_page' => 100,
            'orderby' => 'date', 'order' => 'DESC', 'ignore_sticky_posts' => true,
            'no_found_rows' => true, 'update_post_meta_cache' => true,
        ]);
        if (!$candidates) { return ''; }
        $views = [];
        $total_views = 0;
        $positive_posts = 0;
        foreach ($candidates as $candidate) {
            $count = self::post_views_in_window_v150297($candidate->ID, self::POST_VIEW_WINDOW_DAYS);
            $views[intval($candidate->ID)] = $count;
            $total_views += $count;
            if ($count > 0) { $positive_posts++; }
        }
        $minimum = max(1, intval(apply_filters('pftk_popularity_minimum_sample', 1, [], $candidates)));
        $use_popularity = ($total_views >= $minimum && $positive_posts >= 1);
        if ($use_popularity) {
            usort($candidates, function($a, $b) use ($views) {
                $va = intval($views[intval($a->ID)] ?? 0);
                $vb = intval($views[intval($b->ID)] ?? 0);
                if ($va !== $vb) { return $vb <=> $va; }
                $title_cmp = strcasecmp((string)get_the_title($a), (string)get_the_title($b));
                if ($title_cmp !== 0) { return $title_cmp; }
                return intval($a->ID) <=> intval($b->ID);
            });
            $posts = array_slice($candidates, 0, 6);
            $kicker = 'Pferderatgeber';
            $heading = 'Meistgelesen im Pferde Atelier';
            $mode = 'popularity-90d';
        } else {
            $posts = [];
            $used_post_ids = [];
            $used_category_ids = [];
            foreach ($candidates as $candidate) {
                $category_ids = wp_get_post_categories($candidate->ID, ['fields' => 'ids']);
                $fresh_category = 0;
                foreach ($category_ids as $category_id) {
                    $category_id = intval($category_id);
                    if ($category_id > 0 && !isset($used_category_ids[$category_id])) { $fresh_category = $category_id; break; }
                }
                if ($fresh_category < 1) { continue; }
                $posts[] = $candidate;
                $used_post_ids[intval($candidate->ID)] = true;
                $used_category_ids[$fresh_category] = true;
                if (count($posts) >= 6) { break; }
            }
            if (count($posts) < 6) {
                foreach ($candidates as $candidate) {
                    if (isset($used_post_ids[intval($candidate->ID)])) { continue; }
                    $posts[] = $candidate;
                    if (count($posts) >= 6) { break; }
                }
            }
            $kicker = 'Pferderatgeber';
            $heading = 'Ausgewählt im Pferde Atelier';
            $mode = 'editorial-new';
        }
        if (!$posts) { return ''; }
        $out = '<section class="pftk-home-section-v150170 pftk-home-post-slider-v150205" id="pftk-start-aktuell" data-pftk-post-mode="'.esc_attr($mode).'"><div class="pftk-home-post-head-v150205"><header class="pftk-home-heading-v150170"><span>'.esc_html($kicker).'</span><h2>'.esc_html($heading).'</h2></header><div class="pftk-home-post-controls-v150205" aria-label="Beiträge durchblättern"><button type="button" class="is-prev" aria-label="Vorherige Beiträge"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M14.5 5.5 8 12l6.5 6.5"/></svg></button><button type="button" class="is-next" aria-label="Weitere Beiträge"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M9.5 5.5 16 12l-6.5 6.5"/></svg></button></div></div><div class="pftk-home-posts-v150170" tabindex="0">';
        foreach ($posts as $post) {
            $image = get_the_post_thumbnail_url($post->ID, 'large');
            $categories = get_the_category($post->ID);
            $category_label = !empty($categories) ? $categories[0]->name : 'Pferderatgeber';
            $out .= '<article><a class="pftk-home-post-image-v150170" href="'.esc_url(get_permalink($post)).'">'.($image ? '<img src="'.esc_url($image).'" alt="" loading="lazy">' : '').'</a><div class="pftk-home-post-copy-v150204"><span class="pftk-home-post-category-v150204">'.esc_html($category_label).'</span><h3><a href="'.esc_url(get_permalink($post)).'">'.esc_html(get_the_title($post)).'</a></h3><p>'.esc_html(wp_trim_words(wp_strip_all_tags(get_the_excerpt($post)), 15, '…')).'</p><a class="pftk-home-more-v150170" href="'.esc_url(get_permalink($post)).'">Weiterlesen</a></div></article>';
        }
        return $out.'</div></section>';
    }

    private static function start_home_banner_v150342() {
        $candidate = '';
        if (function_exists('shortcode_exists') && shortcode_exists('pp_affiliate_slot')) {
            $candidate = trim((string)do_shortcode('[pp_affiliate_slot type="start_after_topics" intent="portal_context"]'));
        }
        $plain = strtolower(wp_strip_all_tags($candidate));
        $is_real = $candidate !== ''
            && preg_match('/<(?:img|picture|iframe|a|video)\b/i', $candidate)
            && strpos($candidate, 'data-ppar-placeholder') === false
            && strpos($candidate, 'ppar-placeholder-slot') === false
            && strpos($candidate, 'pftk-affiliate-slot-debug') === false
            && strpos($candidate, 'pftk-affiliate-placeholder') === false
            && strpos($plain, 'affiliate-werbefläche') === false
            && strpos($plain, 'affiliate-werbeflaeche') === false;
        if ($is_real) {
            return '<section class="pftk-home-ad-wide-v150172 is-real-partner-v150342" aria-label="Partnerbanner">'.$candidate.'</section>';
        }
        return '<div class="pftk-home-ad-wide-v150172 is-direct-fallback-v150342">'.self::start_ad_slot_v150162('home-wide-01', 'startseite-nach-hauptkategorien', 'wide').'</div>';
    }

    public static function startpage_cleanup_v150199() {
        if (is_admin() || !is_front_page()) { return; }
        ?>
<script id="pftk-start-cleanup-v150199">
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){
  var root=document.querySelector('.pftk-home-v150170');
  if(!root){return;}
  document.querySelectorAll('h1,h2,h3,h4').forEach(function(h){
    if(root.contains(h)){return;}
    if((h.textContent||'').trim().toLowerCase()!=='aktuelle beiträge'){return;}
    var box=h.closest('section,article,.wp-block-group,.entry-content>div');
    if(box){box.remove();}
  });
  var slider=root.querySelector('.pftk-home-post-slider-v150205 .pftk-home-posts-v150170');
  var prev=root.querySelector('.pftk-home-post-controls-v150205 .is-prev');
  var next=root.querySelector('.pftk-home-post-controls-v150205 .is-next');
  function sliderStep(){
    if(!slider){return 0;}
    var card=slider.querySelector('article');
    if(!card){return 0;}
    var gap=parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(slider).columnGap)||16;
    return card.getBoundingClientRect().width+gap;
  }
  function updateSliderButtons(){
    if(!slider||!prev||!next){return;}
    var max=Math.max(0,slider.scrollWidth-slider.clientWidth-2);
    prev.disabled=slider.scrollLeft<=2;
    next.disabled=slider.scrollLeft>=max;
  }
  if(slider&&prev&&next){
    prev.addEventListener('click',function(){slider.scrollBy({left:-sliderStep(),behavior:'smooth'});});
    next.addEventListener('click',function(){slider.scrollBy({left:sliderStep(),behavior:'smooth'});});
    slider.addEventListener('scroll',function(){window.requestAnimationFrame(updateSliderButtons);},{passive:true});
    updateSliderButtons();
  }
  function syncPostTitles(){
    var titles=Array.prototype.slice.call(root.querySelectorAll('.pftk-home-post-copy-v150204 h3'));
    titles.forEach(function(t){t.style.minHeight='0px';});
    if(window.matchMedia('(max-width:720px)').matches){return;}
    var max=0;titles.forEach(function(t){max=Math.max(max,t.getBoundingClientRect().height);});
    titles.forEach(function(t){t.style.minHeight=Math.ceil(max)+'px';});
  }
  syncPostTitles();
  var rt;window.addEventListener('resize',function(){clearTimeout(rt);rt=setTimeout(function(){syncPostTitles();updateSliderButtons();},120);});
});
</script>
        <?php
    }

    private static function render_guide($type) {
        $title = self::text($type, 'guide_title');
        $intro = self::text($type, 'guide_intro');
        $cards = [
            ['01', self::text($type, 'guide_1_title'), self::text($type, 'guide_1_text'), '#pftk-topics'],
            ['02', self::text($type, 'guide_2_title'), self::text($type, 'guide_2_text'), '#pftk-posts'],
            ['03', self::text($type, 'guide_3_title'), self::text($type, 'guide_3_text'), '#pftk-structure'],
        ];
        $has = false;
        foreach ($cards as $c) { if ($c[1] !== '' || $c[2] !== '') { $has = true; } }
        if ($title === '' && $intro === '' && !$has) { return ''; }

        $out = '<section class="pftk-section" id="pftk-guide"><div class="pftk-section-head">' .
            ($title !== '' ? '<h2 class="pftk-heading pftk-heading-guide_title">' . esc_html($title) . '</h2>' : '') .
            ($intro !== '' ? '<p>' . esc_html($intro) . '</p>' : '') .
            '</div><div class="pftk-guide-grid">';
        foreach ($cards as $i => $c) {
            if ($c[1] === '' && $c[2] === '') { continue; }
            $out .= '<a class="pftk-card pftk-card-guide pftk-curve" style="--pftk-accent:' . esc_attr(self::color($i)) . '" href="' . esc_url($c[3]) . '">' .
                '<span class="pftk-number">' . esc_html($c[0]) . '</span>' .
                ($c[1] !== '' ? '<strong class="pftk-heading pftk-heading-card_title">' . esc_html($c[1]) . '</strong>' : '') .
                ($c[2] !== '' ? '<em>' . esc_html($c[2]) . '</em>' : '') .
                '</a>';
        }
        return $out . '</div></section>';
    }

    private static function render_posts($type, $ctx) {
        $cat_ids = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_map('intval', (array)($ctx['cat_ids'] ?? [])))));
        $selection_mode = self::block($type, 'posts_selection_mode');
        $limit = intval(self::block($type, 'posts_limit'));
        $title = self::text($type, 'posts_title');
        $intro = self::text($type, 'posts_intro');

        // Hub Ebene 1 und 2: meistgelesene Beiträge aus allen passenden Kategorien.
        // Die Kategoriequelle wird aus realen Menüvorkommen und Seitenhierarchie
        // zusammengeführt; das Veröffentlichungsdatum ist kein Sortierkriterium.
        if (in_array($type, ['hub1', 'hub2'], true)) {
            $cat_ids = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_map('intval', (array)($ctx['hub_post_cat_ids'] ?? $cat_ids)))));
            $hub_limit = max(3, min(6, intval(self::block($type, 'posts_limit'))));
            $posts = self::top_posts_by_importance($cat_ids, $hub_limit);
            $raw_title = trim((string)self::raw_text($type, 'posts_title'));
            $raw_intro = trim((string)self::raw_text($type, 'posts_intro'));
            if ($raw_title === '' || $raw_title === 'Wichtige Ratgeber' || $raw_title === 'Ratgeber aus diesem Bereich') { $title = 'Meistgelesene Beiträge aus diesem Bereich'; }
            if ($raw_intro === '' || $raw_intro === 'Automatisch ausgewählte Beiträge mit breiter Themen- und Suchintent-Abdeckung statt nur neueste Beiträge.') {
                $intro = 'Beliebte Beiträge aus diesem Bereich – auf Basis der Aufrufe der letzten 90 Tage.';
            }
            $selection_mode = 'popularity-90d-with-editorial-fallback';
            $limit = $hub_limit;
        } else {
            $posts = self::top_posts_from_categories($cat_ids, $limit, $selection_mode);
        }

        $debug = '';
        if (current_user_can('manage_options') && in_array($type, ['hub1','hub2'], true)) {
            $selected = [];
            foreach ((array)$posts as $debug_post) {
                if ($debug_post instanceof WP_Post) { $selected[] = intval($debug_post->ID); }
            }
            $debug = '<!-- pftk-v' . esc_html(self::VERSION) . ' hub_posts type=' . esc_html($type) .
                ' mode=' . esc_html($selection_mode) . ' cat_count=' . intval(count($cat_ids)) .
                ' post_count=' . intval(count((array)$posts)) . ' cat_ids=' . esc_html(implode(',', $cat_ids)) .
                ' selected=' . esc_html(implode(',', $selected)) . ' -->';
        }

        if (empty($posts) && self::text($type, 'empty_text') === '') { return $debug; }
        $out = '<section class="pftk-section" id="pftk-posts"><div class="pftk-section-head">' .
            ($title !== '' ? '<h2 class="pftk-heading pftk-heading-posts_title">' . esc_html($title) . '</h2>' : '') .
            ($intro !== '' ? '<p>' . esc_html($intro) . '</p>' : '') .
            '</div>';
        if (empty($posts)) { return $debug . $out . self::empty_box($type) . '</section>'; }
        $count_class = 'pftk-card-grid-count-' . intval(count((array)$posts));
        $out .= '<div class="pftk-card-grid ' . esc_attr($count_class) . '">';
        $n = 1;
        foreach ($posts as $post) {
            $excerpt = self::clean_excerpt($post);
            $out .= '<a class="pftk-card pftk-card-post pftk-curve" style="--pftk-accent:' . esc_attr(self::color($n - 1)) . '" href="' . esc_url(get_permalink($post)) . '">' .
                '<span class="pftk-number">' . esc_html(str_pad((string)$n, 2, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT)) . '</span>' .
                '<strong class="pftk-heading pftk-heading-card_title">' . esc_html(get_the_title($post)) . '</strong>' .
                ($excerpt ? '<em>' . esc_html($excerpt) . '</em>' : '') .
                '<b>' . esc_html(self::article_excerpt_cta_v150325()) . '</b>' .
                '</a>';
            $n++;
        }
        return $debug . $out . '</div></section>';
    }

    private static function render_product_articles_after_category_tiles($type, $ctx) {
        $force_articles = !empty($ctx['force_product_articles']);
        if (!$force_articles && !self::visible('category', 'show_product_articles_after_category_tiles')) { return ''; }
        $cat_ids = !empty($ctx['cat_ids']) ? (array)$ctx['cat_ids'] : self::category_ids_from_items($ctx['top_items'] ?? []);
        $cat_ids = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_map('intval', $cat_ids))));
        $limit = self::block('category', 'product_articles_limit') === '8' ? 8 : 4;
        $current = $ctx['current_item'] ?? null;

        // V2.5 / Design 1.16.0:
        // Ebene-3-Artikel duerfen nicht nur von Menue-/Shortcode-Kontext oder
        // existierenden cat_ids abhaengen. Die Logik nutzt zuerst die konkret
        // erkannten Ebene-4-Kacheln/Kategorien und faellt dann auf harte
        // Produktschluessel der aktuellen Seite zurueck, z. B.
        // Fuetterung > Frostschutz > Frostschutzkabel -> Frostschutzkabel.
        $posts = array_slice(self::top_posts_for_product_context($cat_ids, $limit, $current), 0, $limit);
        $debug = current_user_can('manage_options') ? self::product_articles_debug_comment($current, $cat_ids, $posts) : '';

        $manual_title = trim((string)self::raw_text('category', 'product_articles_title'));
        $title = $manual_title !== '' ? $manual_title : self::automatic_product_articles_title($current);
        $intro = trim((string)self::raw_text('category', 'product_articles_intro'));
        if (empty($posts)) {
            if (current_user_can('manage_options')) {
                return $debug . '<section class="pftk-section pftk-product-articles pftk-product-articles-empty" id="pftk-product-articles"><div class="pftk-section-head">' .
                    ($title !== '' ? '<h2 class="pftk-heading pftk-heading-product_articles_title">' . esc_html($title) . '</h2>' : '') .
                    '</div><div class="pftk-empty">Artikelblock aktiv, aber keine passenden sichtbaren Beiträge gefunden.</div></section>';
            }
            return $debug;
        }
        $out = $debug . '<section class="pftk-section pftk-product-articles" id="pftk-product-articles"><div class="pftk-section-head">' .
            ($title !== '' ? '<h2 class="pftk-heading pftk-heading-product_articles_title">' . esc_html($title) . '</h2>' : '') .
            ($intro !== '' ? '<p>' . esc_html($intro) . '</p>' : '') .
            '</div><div class="pftk-card-grid pftk-card-grid-count-' . intval(count((array)$posts)) . '" data-pftk-card-count="' . esc_attr((string)count((array)$posts)) . '">';
        $n = 1;
        foreach ($posts as $post) {
            $excerpt = self::clean_excerpt($post);
            $out .= '<a class="pftk-card pftk-card-post pftk-curve" style="--pftk-accent:' . esc_attr(self::color($n - 1)) . '" href="' . esc_url(get_permalink($post)) . '">' .
                '<span class="pftk-number">' . esc_html(str_pad((string)$n, 2, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT)) . '</span>' .
                '<strong class="pftk-heading pftk-heading-card_title">' . esc_html(get_the_title($post)) . '</strong>' .
                ($excerpt ? '<em>' . esc_html($excerpt) . '</em>' : '') .
                '<b>' . esc_html(self::article_excerpt_cta_v150325()) . '</b>' .
                '</a>';
            $n++;
        }
        return $out . '</div></section>';
    }

    private static function product_articles_debug_comment($current, $cat_ids, $posts) {
        $title = is_object($current) && !empty($current->title) ? (string)$current->title : '';
        $keys = self::category_tile_product_keys_for_current($current);
        return '<!-- pftk-v' . esc_html(self::VERSION) . ' product_articles current=' . esc_html($title) .
            ' keys=' . esc_html(implode(',', array_slice($keys, 0, 8))) .
            ' cat_ids=' . esc_html(implode(',', array_map('intval', (array)$cat_ids))) .
            ' count=' . intval(count((array)$posts)) . ' -->';
    }

    private static function top_posts_for_product_context($cat_ids, $limit, $current = null) {
        $limit = max(1, min(50, intval($limit)));
        $picked = [];
        $seen = [];

        if (!empty($cat_ids)) {
            foreach (self::top_posts_by_importance($cat_ids, $limit) as $post) {
                if ($post instanceof WP_Post && empty($seen[$post->ID])) { $picked[] = $post; $seen[$post->ID] = true; }
                if (count($picked) >= $limit) { return $picked; }
            }
        }

        // Fallback fuer Seiten, bei denen Kategorien zwar semantisch existieren,
        // aber nicht sauber als cat_ids durchgereicht werden: suche publizierte
        // Beitraege ueber starke Produktschluessel im Titel/Slug. Begrenzt und
        // score-sortiert, damit es auch bei vielen Beitraegen beherrschbar bleibt.
        $keys = self::category_tile_product_keys_for_current($current);
        $fallback_posts = self::posts_by_product_keys($keys, max(24, $limit * 4));
        foreach ($fallback_posts as $post) {
            if ($post instanceof WP_Post && empty($seen[$post->ID])) { $picked[] = $post; $seen[$post->ID] = true; }
            if (count($picked) >= $limit) { b\n\n========== EXACT HIT ==========\n\nPATTERN=pftk-post\nOFFSET=1253379\n    * V1.50.376: Ausschliesslich der separate Renderer eines geoeffneten,
     * untersten normalen WordPress-Kategoriearchivs darf das von der
     * Bildzentrale gepflegte Termbild oberhalb des vorhandenen Intro-Kickers
     * ausgeben. Ohne gueltiges thumbnail_id bleibt der bisherige Renderer
     * vollstaendig unveraendert und erzeugt weder Markup noch Leerraum.
     */
    private static function leaf_category_term_image_markup_v150376($term) {
        if (!is_object($term) || (string)($term->taxonomy ?? '') !== 'category') { return ''; }
        $term_id = absint($term->term_id ?? 0);
        if ($term_id <= 0) { return ''; }

        $attachment_id = absint(get_term_meta($term_id, 'thumbnail_id', true));
        if ($attachment_id <= 0 || get_post_type($attachment_id) !== 'attachment' || !wp_attachment_is_image($attachment_id)) { return ''; }

        $full = wp_get_attachment_image_src($attachment_id, 'full');
        if (!is_array($full) || empty($full[0])) { return ''; }

        $src = (string)$full[0];
        $width = absint($full[1] ?? 0);
        $height = absint($full[2] ?? 0);
        if ($src === '' || $width <= 0 || $height <= 0) { return ''; }

        $alt = trim((string)get_post_meta($attachment_id, '_wp_attachment_image_alt', true));
        if ($alt === '') { $alt = trim((string)get_the_title($attachment_id)); }

        $image = '<img src="' . esc_url($src) . '" width="' . intval($width) . '" height="' . intval($height) . '" alt="' . esc_attr($alt) . '" class="pa376-category-image-element" loading="eager" decoding="async" fetchpriority="high">';

        return '<figure class="pa376-category-image" data-pa376-term-image="' . intval($attachment_id) . '">' . $image . '</figure>';
    }

    public static function render_leaf_category_archive_v150273() {
        $term = get_queried_object();
        if (!$term || empty($term->term_id)) { return ''; }
        $term_id=absint($term->term_id);
        $t=self::leaf_category_profile_v150273($term);
        $posts=self::leaf_category_posts_v150273($term_id,30);
        $affiliate_banner = self::leaf_category_real_banner_v150331($term_id);
        $affiliate_products = self::leaf_category_real_products_v150331($term_id);

        // V1.50.335 – gleiche Sichtpruefung wie auf den bestaetigten hoeheren
        // Ebenen: Administratoren sehen bei leerer realer Befuellung eine neutrale
        // Vorschau an der endgueltigen Position. Ausgeloggte Besucher sehen weder
        // Vorschau noch Ueberschrift noch Leerraum.
        $admin_preview_count = self::category_affiliate_admin_preview_count_v150308();
        if ($affiliate_banner === '' && $admin_preview_count > 0) {
            $affiliate_banner = self::leaf_category_banner_preview_v150335();
        }
        if (!$affiliate_products && $admin_preview_count > 0) {
            for ($index = 1; $index <= $admin_preview_count; $index++) {
                $affiliate_products[] = self::leaf_category_product_preview_v150335($index);
            }
        }
        $affiliate_product_count = count($affiliate_products);
        $category_image = self::leaf_category_term_image_markup_v150376($term);

        $leaf_image_class = $category_image !== '' ? ' pa273-leaf-archive-has-image-v150385' : '';
        $intro_image_class = $category_image !== '' ? ' pa273-intro-has-image-v150385' : '';
        $out='<main id="primary" class="site-main pa273-leaf-archive'.$leaf_image_class.'" data-pa335-leaf-only="1">';
        $out.='<section class="pa273-intro'.$intro_image_class.'">'.$category_image.'<span>'.esc_html($t['kicker']).'</span><h1>'.esc_html($t['title']).'</h1>'.self::deck_intro_markup_v150390($t['intro']).'</section>';

        // V1.50.335 – Partnerbereich ausnahmslos oberhalb der Beitragskarten.
        // Oeffentlich nur bei realer Befuellung; Admin-Vorschau nur eingeloggt.
        if ($affiliate_banner !== '') {
            $out.='<section class="pa273-section pa335-partner-section"><header><span>'.esc_html($t['banner_kicker']).'</span><h2>'.esc_html($t['banner_title']).'</h2></header><div class="pa273-banner-slot is-real">'.$affiliate_banner.'</div></section>';
        }

        $out.='<section class="pa273-section pa277-posts-section">';
        if (!$posts) {
            $out.='<div class="pa273-empty">Zu '.esc_html($t['title']).' sind aktuell noch keine Beiträge veröffentlicht.</div>';
        } else {
            $out.='<div class="pa273-post-grid count-' . intval(count((array)$posts)) . '" data-pftk-post-card-count="' . intval(count((array)$posts)) . '">';
            foreach ($posts as $post) {
                $out.=self::render_leaf_post_card_v150272($post);
            }
            $out.='</div>';
        }
        $out.='</section>';

        // V1.50.335 – Produktvorschlaege ausnahmslos unterhalb der Beitragskarten.
        // Oeffentlich nur 1–3 reale Produkte; Admin-Vorschau nur eingeloggt.
        if ($affiliate_product_count > 0) {
            $out.='<section class="pa273-section pa335-products-section"><header><span>'.esc_html($t['products_kicker']).'</span><h2>'.esc_html($t['products_title']).'</h2>'.(trim((string)$t['products_intro']) !== '' ? '<p>'.esc_html($t['products_intro']).'</p>' : '').'</header><div class="pa273-product-grid count-'.intval($affiliate_product_count).'" data-pa335-product-count="'.intval($affiliate_product_count).'">'.implode('', $affiliate_products).'</div></section>';
        }

        // V1.50.360 – Der direkt buchbare Werbeplatz ist auch auf echten
        // Kategoriearchiven wieder sichtbar und nutzt den Startseiten-Renderer.

        $out.='</main>';

            .'.pa273-intro{border-top:1px solid rgba(53,66,42,.24);border-bottom:1px solid rgba(53,66,42,.24);padding:32px 0;text-align:center}'

    public static function enforce_generated_table_follow_gap_v150469() {
        if (is_admin() || !is_singular('post')) { return; }
        ?>
<style id="pftk-generated-table-follow-gap-v150469">
html body.single-post .entry-content .pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469{
  display:block!important;
  box-sizing:border-box!important;
  width:100%!important;
  height:28px!important;
  min-height:28px!important;
  margin:0!important;
  padding:0!important;
  border:0!important;
  background:transparent!important;
  clear:both!important;
  pointer-events:none!important;
}
</style>
<script id="pftk-generated-table-follow-gap-v150469-js">
(function(){
  var entry=document.querySelector('body.single-post .entry-content');
  if(!entry)return;

  function generatedTable(table){
    if(!table||table.tagName!=='TABLE')return false;
    if(table.matches('.system-129-table,.comparison-table'))return true;
    if(table.closest('article.ppm-generated,article[data-article-type]'))return true;
    if(table.closest('section[data-block="table"]'))return true;
    if(table.closest('.pm-table-wrap'))return true;
    if(table.matches('.comparison-table')&&table.querySelector('[data-fact-ids]'))return true;
    return false;
  }

  function outerBlock(table){
    var candidates=['.pm-table-wrap.wp-block-table','.comparison-table-wrap','figure.wp-block-table','.wp-block-table'];
    for(var i=0;i<candidates.length;i++){
      var n=table.closest(candidates[i]);
      if(n&&entry.contains(n))return n;
    }
    return table;
  }

  function nextElementAfter(node){
    var cur=node;
    while(cur&&cur!==entry){
      if(cur.nextElementSibling)return cur.nextElementSibling;
      cur=cur.parentElement;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function directFollowingParagraph(block){
    var next=nextElementAfter(block);
    if(!next)return null;
    if(next.tagName==='P')return next;
    // Nur transparente Einzelelement-Wrapper durchlaufen; niemals in den naechsten
    // semantischen Abschnitt hineinwandern. H2/H3 und Fazit bleiben unangetastet.
    if(next.matches('div:empty'))return null;
    if(next.matches('section,[data-block],h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6'))return null;
    var p=next.querySelector&&next.querySelector(':scope > p:first-child');
    return p||null;
  }

  function apply(){
    entry.querySelectorAll('table').forEach(function(table){
      if(!generatedTable(table))return;
      var block=outerBlock(table);
      var p=directFollowingParagraph(block);
      if(!p)return;
      block.style.setProperty('margin-bottom','0','important');
      var prev=p.previousElementSibling;
      if(prev&&prev.classList.contains('pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469'))return;
      var spacer=document.createElement('div');
      spacer.className='pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469';
      spacer.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
      p.parentNode.insertBefore(spacer,p);
    });
  }

  apply();
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',apply,{once:true});
  window.addEventListener('load',apply,{once:true});
  new MutationObserver(apply).observe(entry,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>
        <?php
    }

    public static function print_universal_content_axis_css() {
        if (is_admin() || is_feed() || is_front_page()) { return; }
        if (!is_singular(['page', 'post']) && !is_category() && !is_tax()) { return; }

    private static function frontend_visible_post_statuses() {
        return current_user_can('edit_posts') ? ['publish','draft','pending','future','private'] : 'publish';
    }

    private static function find_menu_id() {
        $locations = get_nav_menu_locations();
        foreach (['primary', 'menu-1', 'main', 'header-menu', 'ast-hf-menu-1'] as $loc) {
            if (!empty($locations[$loc])) { return intval($locations[$loc]); }
        }
        if (!empty($locations)) { return intval(reset($locations)); }
        $menus = wp_get_nav_menus();
        if (!empty($menus)) { return intval($menus[0]->term_id); }
        return 0;
    }

    private static function find_current_item($items) {
        $page_id = get_the_ID();
        if ($page_id) {
            foreach ($items as $item) {
                if (in_array($item->object, ['page', 'post'], true) && intval($item->object_id) === intval($page_id)) {
                    return $item;
                }
            }
        }
        if (is_category()) {
            $term = get_queried_object();
            if ($term && !empty($term->term_id)) {
                foreach ($items as $item) {
                    if ($item->object === 'category' && intval($item->object_id) === intval($term->term_id)) { return $item; }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static function descendants($parent_id, $children_map) {
        $out = [];
        if (empty($children_map[$parent_id])) { return $out; }
        foreach ($children_map[$parent_id] as $child) {
            $out[] = $child;
            $out = array_merge($out, self::descendants($child->ID, $children_map));
        }
        return $out;
    }

    private static function all_menu_cat_ids($items) {
        $ids = [];
        foreach ($items as $item) { if ($item->object === 'category') { $ids[] = intval($item->object_id); } }
        return array_values(array_unique($ids));
    }

    private static function sort_items($items) {
        usort($items, function($a, $b) {
            $sa = self::item_score($a); $sb = self::item_score($b);
            if ($sa === $sb) { return intval($a->menu_order) <=> intval($b->menu_order); }
            return $sb <=> $sa;
        });
        return $items;
    }

    private static function post_view_day_v150297() {
        return wp_date('Y-m-d');
    }

    private static function post_view_day_timestamp_v150297($day) {
        $day = sanitize_text_field((string)$day);
        $dt = DateTimeImmutable::createFromFormat('!Y-m-d', $day, wp_timezone());
        return $dt instanceof DateTimeImmutable ? $dt->getTimestamp() : false;
    }

    private static function post_view_token_v150297($post_id, $day) {
        return hash_hmac('sha256', absint($post_id) . '|' . sanitize_text_field((string)$day), wp_salt('nonce'));
    }

    private static function post_view_is_bot_v150297($user_agent) {
        $ua = strtolower((string)$user_agent);
        if ($ua === '') { return false; }
        return (bool)preg_match('/bot|crawl|spider|slurp|bingpreview|facebookexternalhit|whatsapp|telegrambot|monitor|uptime|headless|lighthouse|pagespeed|preview/i', $ua);
    }

    public static function render_post_view_beacon_v150297() {
        if (is_admin() || is_feed() || is_preview() || !is_singular('post')) { return; }
        $post_id = absint(get_queried_object_id());
        if ($post_id < 1 || current_user_can('edit_post', $post_id)) { return; }
        $post = get_post($post_id);
        if (!$post instanceof WP_Post || $post->post_status !== 'publish') { return; }
        $day = self::post_view_day_v150297();
        $data = [
            'endpoint' => admin_url('admin-ajax.php'),
            'postId' => $post_id,
            'day' => $day,
            'token' => self::post_view_token_v150297($post_id, $day),
        ];
        echo '<script id="pftk-post-view-v150297">(function(){"use strict";var c=' . wp_json_encode($data, JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE) . ';if(!c||!c.postId){return;}var sent=false;function send(){if(sent||document.visibilityState==="hidden"){return;}sent=true;var f=new FormData();f.append("action","pftk_record_post_view");f.append("post_id",String(c.postId));f.append("day",c.day);f.append("token",c.token);try{if(navigator.sendBeacon&&navigator.sendBeacon(c.endpoint,f)){return;}}catch(e){}fetch(c.endpoint,{method:"POST",body:f,credentials:"same-origin",keepalive:true}).catch(function(){});}window.setTimeout(send,3000);})();</script>';
    }

    public static function ajax_record_post_view_v150297() {
        nocache_headers();
        $post_id = absint($_POST['post_id'] ?? 0);
        $day = sanitize_text_field(wp_unslash($_POST['day'] ?? ''));
        $token = sanitize_text_field(wp_unslash($_POST['token'] ?? ''));
        $post = $post_id > 0 ? get_post($post_id) : null;
        if (!$post instanceof WP_Post || $post->post_type !== 'post' || $post->post_status !== 'publish') {
            wp_send_json_error(['reason' => 'invalid_post'], 400);
        }
        if (current_user_can('edit_post', $post_id) || self::post_view_is_bot_v150297($_SERVER['HTTP_USER_AGENT'] ?? '')) {
            wp_send_json_success(['counted' => false]);
        }
        $today = self::post_view_day_v150297();
        $yesterday = current_datetime()->modify('-1 day')->format('Y-m-d');
        $valid = ($day === $today && hash_equals(self::post_view_token_v150297($post_id, $today), $token))
            || ($day === $yesterday && hash_equals(self::post_view_token_v150297($post_id, $yesterday), $token));
        if (!$valid) { wp_send_json_error(['reason' => 'invalid_token'], 403); }
        $referer = wp_get_referer();
        if ($referer) {
            $home_host = strtolower((string)wp_parse_url(home_url('/'), PHP_URL_HOST));
            $ref_host = strtolower((string)wp_parse_url($referer, PHP_URL_HOST));
            if ($home_host === '' || $ref_host !== $home_host) { wp_send_json_error(['reason' => 'invalid_referer'], 403); }
        }
        $fingerprint = hash_hmac('sha256', $post_id . '|' . ($_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR'] ?? '') . '|' . ($_SERVER['HTTP_USER_AGENT'] ?? ''), wp_salt('auth'));
        $transient = 'pftk_view_' . substr($fingerprint, 0, 32);
        if (get_transient($transient)) { wp_send_json_success(['counted' => false]); }
        set_transient($transient, 1, self::POST_VIEW_DEDUPE_SECONDS);

        $daily = get_post_meta($post_id, self::META_POST_VIEWS_DAILY, true);
        if (!is_array($daily)) { $daily = []; }
        $cutoff = current_datetime()->getTimestamp() - ((self::POST_VIEW_WINDOW_DAYS + 7) * DAY_IN_SECONDS);
        $clean = [];
        foreach ($daily as $stored_day => $count) {
            $stored_day = sanitize_text_field((string)$stored_day);
            $ts = self::post_view_day_timestamp_v150297($stored_day);
            if ($ts !== false && $ts >= $cutoff) { $clean[$stored_day] = max(0, intval($count)); }
        }
        $clean[$today] = intval($clean[$today] ?? 0) + 1;
        update_post_meta($post_id, self::META_POST_VIEWS_DAILY, $clean);
        update_post_meta($post_id, self::META_POST_VIEWS_90D, self::post_views_from_daily_v150297($clean, self::POST_VIEW_WINDOW_DAYS));
        wp_send_json_success(['counted' => true]);
    }

    private static function post_views_from_daily_v150297($daily, $days = 90) {
        $days = max(1, intval($days));
        if (!is_array($daily)) { return 0; }
        $cutoff = current_datetime()->getTimestamp() - (($days - 1) * DAY_IN_SECONDS);
        $total = 0;
        foreach ($daily as $day => $count) {
            $ts = self::post_view_day_timestamp_v150297($day);
            if ($ts !== false && $ts >= $cutoff) { $total += max(0, intval($count)); }
        }
        return $total;
    }

    private static function post_views_in_window_v150297($post_id, $days = 90) {
        $daily = get_post_meta(absint($post_id), self::META_POST_VIEWS_DAILY, true);
        return self::post_views_from_daily_v150297(is_array($daily) ? $daily : [], $days);
    }

    private static function item_score($item) {
        $score = 0;
        if (in_array($item->object, ['page','post'], true)) { $score = intval(get_post_meta(intval($item->object_id), 'pft_affiliate_score', true)); }
        elseif ($item->object === 'category') { $score = intval(get_term_meta(intval($item->object_id), 'pft_affiliate_score', true)); }
        $title = mb_strtolower((string)$item->title);
        foreach (['vergleich'=>25,'test'=>22,'beste'=>22,'kaufberatung'=>25,'kosten'=>16,'preis'=>16,'kaufen'=>18,'erfahrung'=>12,'faq'=>4,'pflege'=>6] as $kw => $add) {
            if (strpos($title, $kw) !== false) { $score += $add; }
        }
        return $score;
    }

    /**
     * Strukturierte Portalbereiche: meistgelesene Beiträge der letzten 90 Tage.
     * Unterkategorien werden rekursiv einbezogen. Bis mindestens zehn gueltige
     * Aufrufe im Kandidatenfeld vorliegen, bleibt die bestehende redaktionelle
     * Gewichtung der belastbare Fallback. Datum ist kein Sortierkriterium.
     */

            $total_views += $count;
        }
        $minimum = max(1, intval(apply_filters('pftk_popularity_minimum_sample', 10, $cat_ids, $posts)));
        $use_popularity = $total_views >= $minimum;
        usort($posts, function($a, $b) use ($views, $use_popularity) {

            . '.pftk-brand-footer-mark-v15065{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:18px;padding:0;color:var(--pa-ochre);background:#fff}'
            . '.pftk-brand-footer-mark-v15065 span{display:block;width:min(110px,11vw);height:1px;background:rgba(200,146,20,.72)}'
            . '.pftk-brand-horseshoe-v15065{width:46px;height:46px}'
            . '.pftk-brand-footer-social-v15065{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:flex-end;gap:14px;min-height:42px}'
            . '.pftk-brand-footer-social-v\n\n========== EXACT HIT ==========\n\nPATTERN=entry-content\nOFFSET=155732\nrd_count = count(preg_split('/\s+/u', $text, -1, PREG_SPLIT_NO_EMPTY));
        if ($word_count < 130) { return '<p>' . esc_html($text) . '</p>'; }
        $sentences = preg_split('/(?<=[.!?])\s+(?=[\p{Lu}\d„])/u', $text, -1, PREG_SPLIT_NO_EMPTY);
        $find_count = 2;
        if (!is_array($sentences) || count($sentences) <= $find_count + 2) { return '<p>' . esc_html($text) . '</p>'; }
        $find = array_splice($sentences, -$find_count);
        $body = array_values($sentences);
        $target = 55;
        $best_i = 1; $best_delta = PHP_INT_MAX;
        for ($i = 1; $i < count($body); $i++) {
            $lead_words = count(preg_split('/\s+/u', trim(implode(' ', array_slice($body, 0, $i))), -1, PREG_SPLIT_NO_EMPTY));
            $more_words = count(preg_split('/\s+/u', trim(implode(' ', array_slice($body, $i))), -1, PREG_SPLIT_NO_EMPTY));
            if ($more_words < 25) { continue; }
            $delta = abs($lead_words - $target);
            if ($delta < $best_delta) { $best_delta = $delta; $best_i = $i; }
        }
        $lead = trim(implode(' ', array_slice($body, 0, $best_i)));
        $more = trim(implode(' ', array_slice($body, $best_i)));
        $find_text = trim(implode(' ', $find));
        if (trim($lead . ' ' . $more . ' ' . $find_text) !== $text || $more === '' || $find_text === '') { return '<p>' . esc_html($text) . '</p>'; }
        $css = '<style id="pa390-intro-fold-css">'
            . '.pa390-intro-copy{width:min(100%,900px)!important;max-width:900px!important;margin:0 auto!important;text-align:left!important}'
            . '.pa390-intro-copy>.pa390-intro-lead{max-width:none!important;margin:0!important;text-align:left!important;line-height:1.72!important}'
            . '.pa390-intro-more{max-width:none!important;margin:10px 0 12px!important;padding:0!important;border:0!important;text-align:center!important}'
            . '.pa390-intro-more>summary{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;cursor:pointer;list-style:none;color:#35422A;font:inherit;font-size:.9em;font-weight:700;line-height:1.3;transition:color .18s ease}'
            . '.pa390-intro-more>summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none}'
            . '.pa390-intro-more>summary:before{content:"";display:inline-block;width:7px;height:7px;border-right:1.5px solid currentColor;border-bottom:1.5px solid currentColor;transform:rotate(45deg) translateY(-1px);transform-origin:center;transition:transform .18s ease}'
            . '.pa390-intro-more[open]>summary:before{transform:rotate(225deg) translate(-1px,-1px)}'
            . '.pa390-intro-more>summary:hover,.pa390-intro-more>summary:focus-visible{color:#C89214;outline:0}'
            . '.pa390-intro-more-text{padding-top:10px;text-align:left!important}'
            . '.pa390-intro-more-text>p{max-width:none!important;margin:0!important;text-align:left!important;line-height:1.72!important}'
            . '.pa390-intro-find{max-width:none!important;margin:18px 0 0!important;padding:14px 16px!important;border:0!important;border-left:3px solid #C89214!important;background:rgba(242,232,214,.34)!important;text-align:left!important;line-height:1.62!important}'
            . '.pa390-intro-find>strong{display:block;margin:0 0 5px;color:#35422A!important;font:inherit;font-weight:700!important;line-height:1.35}'
            . '.pa390-intro-find>p{max-width:none!important;margin:0!important;text-align:left!important;line-height:1.62!important}'
            . '.pa255-intro:has(.pa390-intro-copy){border-top:0!important;padding-top:4px!important}'
            . '.pa266-category .pa266-intro:has(.pa390-intro-copy),.pa333-leaf-page .pa272-intro:has(.pa390-intro-copy),.pa273-leaf-archive .pa273-intro:has(.pa390-intro-copy){border-top:0!important;padding-top:4px!important}'
            . 'body:not(.pftk-has-leading-page-image-v150385):not(.pftk-has-leading-term-image-v150385):has(.pa390-intro-copy) .pftk-universal-breadcrumb-wrap.pftk-breadcrumb-mounted{margin-bottom:0!important}'
            . 'body:not(.pftk-has-leading-page-image-v150385):not(.pftk-has-leading-term-image-v150385):has(.pa390-intro-copy) .content-area,body:not(.pftk-has-leading-page-image-v150385):not(.pftk-has-leading-term-image-v150385):has(.pa390-intro-copy) .site-main,body:not(.pftk-has-leading-page-image-v150385):not(.pftk-has-leading-term-image-v150385):has(.pa390-intro-copy) article.page,body:not(.pftk-has-leading-page-image-v150385):not(.pftk-has-leading-term-image-v150385):has(.pa390-intro-copy) article.page>.entry-content{margin-top:0!important;padding-top:0!important}'
            .             .
