            . '@media(max-width:620px){.pa390-intro-copy{max-width:100%!important}.pa390-intro-find{padding:12px 14px!important}.pa255-intro:has(.pa390-intro-copy){padding-top:2px!important}.pa266-category .pa266-intro:has(.pa390-intro-copy),.pa333-leaf-page .pa272-intro:has(.pa390-intro-copy),.pa273-leaf-archive .pa273-intro:has(.pa390-intro-copy){padding-top:2px!important}}'
            . '@media(max-width:544px){body:not(.pftk-has-leading-page-image-v150385):not(.pftk-has-leading-term-image-v150385):has(.pa390-intro-copy) .pftk-universal-breadcrumb-wrap.pftk-breadcrumb-mounted{margin-bottom:0!important}}'
            . '</style>';

            . '<p class="pa390-intro-lead">' . esc_html($lead) . '</p>'
            . '<details class="pa390-intro-more"><summary>Mehr erfahren</summary><div class="pa390-intro-more-text"><p>' . esc_html($more) . '</p></div></details>'
            . '<div class="pa390-intro-find"><strong>Was du hier findest</strong><p>' . esc_html($find_text) . '</p></div>'
            . '</div>';

    public static function generate_category_intro_text($page_id, $type = '') {
        $page_id = absint($page_id);
        $post = get_post($page_id);
        if (!$post instanceof WP_Post || $post->post_type !== 'page' || $post->post_status !== 'publish') { return ''; }
        $type = $type !== '' ? sanitize_key($type) : self::page_type_for_id($page_id);
        if (!in_array($type, ['start','hub1','hub2','category'], true)) { return ''; }
        $settings = self::category_text_settings();
        if (!self::category_text_type_enabled($type, $settings)) { return ''; }

        $brand = trim((string)$settings['brand_name']) ?: 'Pferde Atelier';
        $audience = trim((string)$settings['audience_phrase']);
        $title = trim((string)get_the_title($post));
        $profile = self::category_text_profile_for_page($page_id, $title);
        $focus = trim((string)($profile['focus'] ?? 'Auswahl und praktische Unterschiede'));
        $criteria = self::human_list((array)($profile['criteria'] ?? []), 4);
        $children = self::category_text_terms_for_page($page_id, $type);
        $child_list = self::human_list($children, $type === 'hub1' ? 4 : 3);
        $parents = self::category_text_parent_titles($page_id, 2);
        $parent = !empty($parents[0]) ? $parents[0] : '';
        $variant = self::category_text_variant($page_id, $title);

        if (in_array($type, ['hub2','category'], true)) {
            $pilot = self::deck_intro_only_v150389('main/' . self::normalize_match_key($title), '');
            if ($pilot !== '') { return $pilot; }
            $editorial = self::category_intro_editorial_page_batch1361_v150427($page_id);
            if ($editorial !== '') { return $editorial; }
            $intent = $type === 'category' ? self::human_list(self::category_text_content_intents_for_page($page_id), 3) : 'Ratgeber, Vergleiche und praktische Hinweise';
            return self::portal_lower_intro_v150396($title, $focus, (array)($profile['criteria'] ?? []), $parent, $children, $intent, 'generated|' . $type . '|' . self::normalize_match_key($parent) . '|' . self::normalize_match_key($title));
        }

        if ($type === 'start') {
            $text = 'Im ' . $brand . ' findest du die wichtigsten Themenwelten rund um ' . ($audience !== '' ? $audience : 'Pferde, Haltung und Reitsport') . '. Ratgeber, Vergleiche und klar gegliederte Kategorien führen dich schnell zu passenden Informationen und Angeboten.';
            $limit = 65;
        } elseif ($type === 'hub1') {
            if ($variant === 0) {
                $text = 'Im ' . $brand . ' führt dich ' . $title . ($child_list !== '' ? ' zu Bereichen wie ' . $child_list : ' zu den passenden Unterthemen') . '. Im Mittelpunkt stehen ' . $focus . '. Ratgeber und Vergleiche helfen dir, ' . $criteria . ' gezielt einzuordnen.';
            } else {
                $text = $title . ' bündelt im ' . $brand . ($child_list !== '' ? ' Themen wie ' . $child_list : ' die wichtigsten Unterbereiche') . '. So findest du schnell Informationen zu ' . $focus . ' und kannst ' . $criteria . ' besser vergleichen.';
            }
            $limit = 90;
        } elseif ($type === 'hub2') {
            if ($variant === 0) {
                $text = $title . ' bündelt im ' . $brand . ' Themen rund um ' . $focus . '.';
                if ($child_list !== '') { $text .= ' Dazu gehören ' . $child_list . '.'; }
                $text .= ' Ratgeber und Vergleiche helfen dir, ' . $criteria . ' besser einzuordnen.';
            } else {
                $text = 'Im Bereich ' . $title . ' geht es um ' . $focus . '. Das ' . $brand . ($child_list !== '' ? ' behandelt Unterthemen wie ' . $child_list : ' zeigt dir die passenden Unterthemen') . ' und erklärt, welche Rolle ' . $criteria . ' bei Auswahl und Nutzung spielen.';
            }
            $limit = 80;
        } else {
            $intents = self::human_list(self::category_text_content_intents_for_page($page_id), 3);
            if ($variant === 0) {
                $text = 'Für ' . $title . ($parent !== '' ? ' im Bereich ' . $parent : '') . ' sind ' . $criteria . ' besonders wichtig. Im ' . $brand . ' findest du dazu ' . $intents . ', die ' . $focus . ' verständlich einordnen.';

'@media(max-width:760px){.pftk-market-intro-v150279{padding:0 10px;margin-bottom:18px}.pftk-market-head-v150279{padding:17px 6px 15px}.pftk-market-kicker-v150279{font-size:9px;gap:8px}.pftk-market-kicker-v150279:before,.pftk-market-kicker-v150279:after{width:24px}.pftk-market-actions-v150279 a{flex:1 1 140px}.pftk-market-partner-preview-v150338{min-height:52px}}'
            . '</style>'
            . $page_image
            . '<div class="pftk-market-head-v150279">'
            . '<div class="pftk-market-kicker-v150279">' . esc_html($atts['kicker']) . '</div>'
            . '<h1 id="pftk-market-title-v150279">' . esc_html($atts['title']) . '</h1>'

    private static function is_journal_page_v150368() {
        if (is_admin()) { return false; }
        if (function_exists('is_page') && is_page('journal')) { return true; }
        $page_id = function_exists('get_queried_object_id') ? absint(get_queried_object_id()) : 0;
        $post = $page_id > 0 && function_exists('get_post') ? get_post($page_id) : null;
        if ($post instanceof WP_Post) {
            if (sanitize_title((string)$post->post_name) === 'journal') { return true; }
            $content = (string)$post->post_content;
            if (function_exists('has_shortcode') && (has_shortcode($content, 'pferde_journal') || has_shortcode($content, 'affiliate_portal_journal'))) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    public static function journal_body_class_v150280($classes) {
        $classes = is_array($classes) ? $classes : [];
        if (self::is_journal_page_v150368()) {
            $classes[] = 'pftk-journal-page-v150280';
            $classes[] = 'pftk-journal-page-v150368';
            $classes[] = 'pftk-journal-page-v150369';
            $classes[] = 'pftk-journal-page-v150371';
        }
        return array_values(array_unique($classes));
    }

    private static function render_hub1_v150210() {
        $page_id = is_singular('page') ? absint(get_queried_object_id()) : 0;
        if (!self::hub1_is_pilot_v150210($page_id)) { return self::render_hub1_legacy_v150198(); }
        $ctx = self::context('hub1');
        $items = self::sort_structural_items(self::published_page_items($ctx['top_items'] ?? [], 0, false));
        $primary = array_slice($items, 0, 6);
        $remaining = array_slice($items, 6);
        $key = self::hub1_category_key_v150210($page_id);
        $hero = self::hub1_hero_config_v150210($page_id, $key);
        if ($page_id > 0) {
            self::$category_intro_rendered[$page_id] = true;
            self::$page_shortline_rendered[$page_id] = true;
        }
        $button_page = self::topic_cta_v150325();
        $used_icons = [];
        ob_start();
        echo '<div class="pftk-hub1-v150210" data-pftk-page-intro="1" data-pftk-page-shortline="1" data-pftk-hub1-category="' . esc_attr($key) . '">';
        echo '<section class="pftk-hub1-hero-v150210"><div class="pftk-hub1-hero-copy-v150210"><span>' . esc_html($hero['shortline']) . '</span><h1>' . esc_html($hero['title']) . '</h1><p>' . esc_html($hero['intro']) . '</p><a href="#pftk-hub1-topics-v150210"><span class="pftk-hub1-button-label-v150221">' . esc_html($hero['button']) . '</span></a></div><figure><img src="' . esc_url($hero['image']) . '" alt="' . esc_attr($hero['alt']) . '" fetchpriority="high"></figure></section>';
        if ($primary) {
            $topics_title = trim((string)self::text('hub1','topics_title'));
            if ($topics_title === '' || $topics_title === 'Ausgewählte Themen') { $topics_title = 'Die wichtigsten Themen rund um ' . $hero['title']; }
            echo '<section class="pftk-hub1-section-v150210 pftk-hub1-topics-v150210" id="pftk-hub1-topics-v150210"><h2>' . esc_html($topics_title) . '</h2><div class="pftk-hub1-topic-grid-v150210">';
            foreach ($primary as $item) {
                $icon_markup = self::hub1_modern_icon_v150222($item->title);
                echo '<a class="pftk-hub1-topic-v150210" href="' . esc_url($item->url) . '">' . $icon_markup . '<h3>' . esc_html($item->title) . '</h3><p>' . esc_html(self::start_category_description_v150161($item->title)) . '</p><span>' . esc_html($button_page) . '</span></a>';
            }
            echo '</div></section>';
        }
        echo self::hub1_products_v150210();
        echo '<section class="pftk-hub1-banner-v150210 pftk-hub1-banner-main-v150210" aria-label="Werbung und Affiliate">' . self::hub1_banner_slot_v150211('hub_after_cards') . '</section>';
        echo self::hub1_posts_v150210($ctx, $hero['title']);
        echo '</div>';
        return ob_get_clean();
    }

        // V1.50.161: Startseite vollständig neu und isoliert. Kein Alt-Renderer,

        }

        $popular_source_ids = !empty($ctx['hub_post_cat_ids']) ? (array)$ctx['hub_post_cat_ids'] : (array)($ctx['cat_ids'] ?? []);
        $popular_cat_ids = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_map('intval', $popular_source_ids))));

        }

    public static function startpage_cleanup_v150199() {
        if (is_admin() || !is_front_page()) { return; }
        ?>
<script id="pftk-start-cleanup-v150199">
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){
  var root=document.querySelector('.pftk-home-v150170');
  if(!root){return;}
  document.querySelectorAll('h1,h2,h3,h4').forEach(function(h){
    if(root.contains(h)){return;}
    if((h.textContent||'').trim().toLowerCase()!=='aktuelle beiträge'){return;}
    var box=h.closest('section,article,.wp-block-group,.entry-content>div');
    if(box){box.remove();}
  });
  var slider=root.querySelector('.pftk-home-post-slider-v150205 .pftk-home-posts-v150170');
  var prev=root.querySelector('.pftk-home-post-controls-v150205 .is-prev');
  var next=root.querySelector('.pftk-home-post-controls-v150205 .is-next');
  function sliderStep(){
    if(!slider){return 0;}
    var card=slider.querySelector('article');
    if(!card){return 0;}
    var gap=parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(slider).columnGap)||16;
    return card.getBoundingClientRect().width+gap;
  }
  function updateSliderButtons(){
    if(!slider||!prev||!next){return;}
    var max=Math.max(0,slider.scrollWidth-slider.clientWidth-2);
    prev.disabled=slider.scrollLeft<=2;
    next.disabled=slider.scrollLeft>=max;
  }
  if(slider&&prev&&next){
    prev.addEventListener('click',function(){slider.scrollBy({left:-sliderStep(),behavior:'smooth'});});
    next.addEventListener('click',function(){slider.scrollBy({left:sliderStep(),behavior:'smooth'});});
    slider.addEventListener('scroll',function(){window.requestAnimationFrame(updateSliderButtons);},{passive:true});
    updateSliderButtons();
  }
  function syncPostTitles(){
    var titles=Array.prototype.slice.call(root.querySelectorAll('.pftk-home-post-copy-v150204 h3'));
    titles.forEach(function(t){t.style.minHeight='0px';});
    if(window.matchMedia('(max-width:720px)').matches){return;}
    var max=0;titles.forEach(function(t){max=Math.max(max,t.getBoundingClientRect().height);});
    titles.forEach(function(t){t.style.minHeight=Math.ceil(max)+'px';});
  }
  syncPostTitles();
  var rt;window.addEventListener('resize',function(){clearTimeout(rt);rt=setTimeout(function(){syncPostTitles();updateSliderButtons();},120);});
});
</script>
        <?php
    }

    private static function render_guide($type) {
        $title = self::text($type, 'guide_title');
        $intro = self::text($type, 'guide_intro');
        $cards = [
            ['01', self::text($type, 'guide_1_title'), self::text($type, 'guide_1_text'), '#pftk-topics'],
            ['02', self::text($type, 'guide_2_title'), self::text($type, 'guide_2_text'), '#pftk-posts'],
            ['03', self::text($type, 'guide_3_title'), self::text($type, 'guide_3_text'), '#pftk-structure'],
        ];
        $has = false;
        foreach ($cards as $c) { if ($c[1] !== '' || $c[2] !== '') { $has = true; } }
        if ($title === '' && $intro === '' && !$has) { return ''; }

        $out = '<section class="pftk-section" id="pftk-guide"><div class="pftk-section-head">' .
            ($title !== '' ? '<h2 class="pftk-heading pftk-heading-guide_title">' . esc_html($title) . '</h2>' : '') .
            ($intro !== '' ? '<p>' . esc_html($intro) . '</p>' : '') .
            '</div><div class="pftk-guide-grid">';
        foreach ($cards as $i => $c) {
            if ($c[1] === '' && $c[2] === '') { continue; }
            $out .= '<a class="pftk-card pftk-card-guide pftk-curve" style="--pftk-accent:' . esc_attr(self::color($i)) . '" href="' . esc_url($c[3]) . '">' .
                '<span class="pftk-number">' . esc_html($c[0]) . '</span>' .
                ($c[1] !== '' ? '<strong class="pftk-heading pftk-heading-card_title">' . esc_html($c[1]) . '</strong>' : '') .
                ($c[2] !== '' ? '<em>' . esc_html($c[2]) . '</em>' : '') .
                '</a>';
        }
        return $out . '</div></section>';
    }

    private static function render_posts($type, $ctx) {
        $cat_ids = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_map('intval', (array)($ctx['cat_ids'] ?? [])))));
        $selection_mode = self::block($type, 'posts_selection_mode');
        $limit = intval(self::block($type, 'posts_limit'));
        $title = self::text($type, 'posts_title');
        $intro = self::text($type, 'posts_intro');

        // Hub Ebene 1 und 2: meistgelesene Beiträge aus allen passenden Kategorien.
        // Die Kategoriequelle wird aus realen Menüvorkommen und Seitenhierarchie
        // zusammengeführt; das Veröffentlichungsdatum ist kein Sortierkriterium.
        if (in_array($type, ['hub1', 'hub2'], true)) {
            $cat_ids = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_map('intval', (array)($ctx['hub_post_cat_ids'] ?? $cat_ids)))));
            $hub_limit = max(3, min(6, intval(self::block($type, 'posts_limit'))));
            $posts = self::top_posts_by_importance($cat_ids, $hub_limit);
            $raw_title = trim((string)self::raw_text($type, 'posts_title'));
            $raw_intro = trim((string)self::raw_text($type, 'posts_intro'));
            if ($raw_title === '' || $raw_title === 'Wichtige Ratgeber' || $raw_title === 'Ratgeber aus diesem Bereich') { $title = 'Meistgelesene Beiträge aus diesem Bereich'; }
            if ($raw_intro === '' || $raw_intro === 'Automatisch ausgewählte Beiträge mit breiter Themen- und Suchintent-Abdeckung statt nur neueste Beiträge.') {
                $intro = 'Beliebte Beiträge aus diesem Bereich – auf Basis der Aufrufe der letzten 90 Tage.';
            }
            $selection_mode = 'popularity-90d-with-editorial-fallback';
            $limit = $hub_limit;
        } else {
            $posts = self::top_posts_from_categories($cat_ids, $limit, $selection_mode);
        }

        $debug = '';
        if (current_user_can('manage_options') && in_array($type, ['hub1','hub2'], true)) {
            $selected = [];
            foreach ((array)$posts as $debug_post) {
                if ($debug_post instanceof WP_Post) { $selected[] = intval($debug_post->ID); }
            }
            $debug = '<!-- pftk-v' . esc_html(self::VERSION) . ' hub_posts type=' . esc_html($type) .
                ' mode=' . esc_html($selection_mode) . ' cat_count=' . intval(count($cat_ids)) .
                ' post_count=' . intval(count((array)$posts)) . ' cat_ids=' . esc_html(implode(',', $cat_ids)) .
                ' selected=' . esc_html(implode(',', $selected)) . ' -->';
        }

        if (empty($posts) && self::text($type, 'empty_text') === '') { return $debug; }
        $out = '<section class="pftk-section" id="pftk-posts"><div class="pftk-section-head">' .
            ($title !== '' ? '<h2 class="pftk-heading pftk-heading-posts_title">' . esc_html($title) . '</h2>' : '') .
            ($intro !== '' ? '<p>' . esc_html($intro) . '</p>' : '') .
            '</div>';
        if (empty($posts)) { return $debug . $out . self::empty_box($type) . '</section>'; }
        $count_class = 'pftk-card-grid-count-' . intval(count\n\n========== EXACT HIT ==========\n\nPATTERN=entry-content\nOFFSET=1267534\nbject_id(), true);
        }
        if (is_category()) {
            $term = get_queried_object();
            if (!is_object($term) || empty($term->term_id)) { return []; }
            $journal_chain = self::journal_category_breadcrumb_chain_v150458($term);
            return !empty($journal_chain) ? $journal_chain : self::breadcrumb_portal_category_chain_v150310($term, true);
        }
        if (is_singular('post')) {
            $post_id = get_queried_object_id();
            $category = self::primary_article_category_v150297($post_id);
            if (is_object($category) && !empty($category->term_id)) {
                // Der aktuelle bindende Vertrag V4.99 endet beim echten Termnamen.
                // Der Beitragstitel bleibt bewusst ausserhalb des Breadcrumbs.
                return self::breadcrumb_portal_category_chain_v150310($category, false);
            }
        }
        return [];
    }

    private static function echo_breadcrumb_once($context) {
        static $rendered = [];
        if (!empty($rendered[$context]) || is_admin() || is_feed() || is_front_page()) { return; }
        $html = self::render_breadcrumb_markup(self::current_breadcrumb_crumbs(), true);
        if ($html === '') { return; }
        $rendered[$context] = true;
        echo '<div class="pftk-universal-breadcrumb-wrap pftk-breadcrumb-' . esc_attr($context) . '">' . $html . '</div>';
    }

    public static function render_singular_breadcrumb() {
        if (!is_singular(['page', 'post'])) { return; }
        self::echo_breadcrumb_once('singular');
    }

    public static function render_category_breadcrumb() {
        if (!is_category()) { return; }
        self::echo_breadcrumb_once('category');
    }


    public static function render_universal_breadcrumb_payload() {
        if (is_admin() || is_feed() || is_front_page()) { return; }
        if (!is_singular(['page', 'post']) && !is_category() && !is_tax()) { return; }
        $html = self::render_breadcrumb_markup(self::current_breadcrumb_crumbs(), true);
        if ($html === '') { return; }
        echo '<div id="pftk-breadcrumb-payload" hidden>' . $html . '</div>';
        echo <<<'HTML'
<script id="pftk-breadcrumb-mount-v150311">
(function(){
  function mount(){
    var payload=document.getElementById('pftk-breadcrumb-payload');
    var siteContent=document.querySelector('.site-content');
    if(!payload||!siteContent){return;}
    var nav=payload.querySelector('.pftk-content-breadcrumb');
    if(!nav){payload.remove();return;}
    var style=payload.querySelector('style');
    var schema=payload.querySelector('script[type="application/ld+json"]');
    if(style){document.head.appendChild(style);}
    if(schema){schema.id='pftk-breadcrumb-jsonld-v150311';document.head.appendChild(schema);}
    var mount=document.createElement('div');
    mount.className='pftk-universal-breadcrumb-wrap pftk-breadcrumb-mounted';
    mount.appendChild(nav);
    siteContent.insertBefore(mount,siteContent.firstChild);
    payload.remove();
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading'){document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',mount,{once:true});}
  else{mount();}
})();
</script>
HTML;
    }

    public static function enqueue_single_post_locked_style() {
        if (is_admin() || !is_singular('post')) { return; }
        wp_enqueue_style(
            'pftk-single-post-locked-v15083',
            plugins_url('assets/single-post-locked-v15083.css', __FILE__),
            ['pferde-template-kit'],
            self::VERSION
        );
    }

     * der Komponenten bleiben unveraendert. Die Startseite bleibt unangetastet.

    /**
     * V1.50.469 / Contract V104 – non-collapsing post-table gap for generated tables.
     *
     * V103 is explicitly NOT accepted as proof: its local reference compared a
     * legacy table->H2 transition with a generated table->paragraph transition.
     * That was an invalid apples-to-oranges geometry test. Live V103 then showed
     * no visible change, so its semantic adjacency selector cannot be treated as
     * a proven representation of the rendered WordPress DOM.
     *
     * V104 removes that dependency. The final browser DOM is inspected after the
     * article-design runtime has executed. Production-table signatures are the
     * table classes themselves (comparison-table/system-129-table), with the
     * semantic/article/wrapper markers retained as additional evidence. When the
     * actual following content node is a paragraph, a real 28px spacer element is
     * inserted. A real element cannot collapse with paragraph margins, unlike the
     * earlier margin-only fixes. Plain legacy Gutenberg tables stay untouched.
     */
    public static function enforce_generated_table_follow_gap_v150469() {
        if (is_admin() || !is_singular('post')) { return; }
        ?>
<style id="pftk-generated-table-follow-gap-v150469">
html body.single-post .entry-content .pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469{
  display:block!important;
  box-sizing:border-box!important;
  width:100%!important;
  height:28px!important;
  min-height:28px!important;
  margin:0!important;
  padding:0!important;
  border:0!important;
  background:transparent!important;
  clear:both!important;
  pointer-events:none!important;
}
</style>
<script id="pftk-generated-table-follow-gap-v150469-js">
(function(){
  var entry=document.querySelector('body.single-post .entry-content');
  if(!entry)return;

  function generatedTable(table){
    if(!table||table.tagName!=='TABLE')return false;
    if(table.matches('.system-129-table,.comparison-table'))return true;
    if(table.closest('article.ppm-generated,article[data-article-type]'))return true;
    if(table.closest('section[data-block="table"]'))return true;
    if(table.closest('.pm-table-wrap'))return true;
    if(table.matches('.comparison-table')&&table.querySelector('[data-fact-ids]'))return true;
    return false;
  }

  function outerBlock(table){
    var candidates=['.pm-table-wrap.wp-block-table','.comparison-table-wrap','figure.wp-block-table','.wp-block-table'];
    for(var i=0;i<candidates.length;i++){
      var n=table.closest(candidates[i]);
      if(n&&entry.contains(n))return n;
    }
    return table;
  }

  function nextElementAfter(node){
    var cur=node;
    while(cur&&cur!==entry){
      if(cur.nextElementSibling)return cur.nextElementSibling;
      cur=cur.parentElement;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function directFollowingParagraph(block){
    var next=nextElementAfter(block);
    if(!next)return null;
    if(next.tagName==='P')return next;
    // Nur transparente Einzelelement-Wrapper durchlaufen; niemals in den naechsten
    // semantischen Abschnitt hineinwandern. H2/H3 und Fazit bleiben unangetastet.
    if(next.matches('div:empty'))return null;
    if(next.matches('section,[data-block],h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6'))return null;
    var p=next.querySelector&&next.querySelector(':scope > p:first-child');
    return p||null;
  }

  function apply(){
    entry.querySelectorAll('table').forEach(function(table){
      if(!generatedTable(table))return;
      var block=outerBlock(table);
      var p=directFollowingParagraph(block);
      if(!p)return;
      block.style.setProperty('margin-bottom','0','important');
      var prev=p.previousElementSibling;
      if(prev&&prev.classList.contains('pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469'))return;
      var spacer=document.createElement('div');
      spacer.className='pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469';
      spacer.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
      p.parentNode.insertBefore(spacer,p);
    });
  }

  apply();
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',apply,{once:true});
  window.addEventListener('load',apply,{once:true});
  new MutationObserver(apply).observe(entry,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>
        <?php
    }

    public static function print_universal_content_axis_css() {
        if (is_admin() || is_feed() || is_front_page()) { return; }
        if (!is_singular(['page', 'post']) && !is_category() && !is_tax()) { return; }
        $layout = self::global_layout_settings();
        $axis = max(620, min(1400, intval($layout['content_axis_width'] ?? 900)));
        $post_width = max(620, min(1000, intval($layout['post_reading_width'] ?? 760)));
        $breadcrumb_width = max(620, min(1400, intval($layout['breadcrumb_axis_width'] ?? 900)));
        $breadcrumb_title_gap = max(0, min(80, intval($layout['breadcrumb_title_gap'] ?? 10)));
        $desktop = max(0, min(160, intval($layout['navigation_content_gap_desktop'] ?? 18)));
        $tablet = max(0, min(160, intval($layout['navigation_content_gap_tablet'] ?? 14)));
        $mobile = max(0, min(160, intval($layout['navigation_content_gap_mobile'] ?? 10)));
        $radius = max(0, min(40, intval($layout['global_card_radius'] ?? 12)));
        $title_image_gap = max(0, min(80, intval($layout['title_image_gap'] ?? 12)));
        echo '<style id="pftk-universal-content-axis-v15061">'
            . ':root{--pftk-content-axis-width:' . esc_attr($axis) . 'px;--pftk-post-reading-width:' . esc_attr($post_wi\n\n========== EXACT HIT ==========\n\nPATTERN=entry-content\nOFFSET=1267679\nerm->term_id)) { return []; }
            $journal_chain = self::journal_category_breadcrumb_chain_v150458($term);
            return !empty($journal_chain) ? $journal_chain : self::breadcrumb_portal_category_chain_v150310($term, true);
        }
        if (is_singular('post')) {
            $post_id = get_queried_object_id();
            $category = self::primary_article_category_v150297($post_id);
            if (is_object($category) && !empty($category->term_id)) {
                // Der aktuelle bindende Vertrag V4.99 endet beim echten Termnamen.
                // Der Beitragstitel bleibt bewusst ausserhalb des Breadcrumbs.
                return self::breadcrumb_portal_category_chain_v150310($category, false);
            }
        }
        return [];
    }

    /**
     * V1.50.469 / Contract V104 – non-collapsing post-table gap for generated tables.
     *
     * V103 is explicitly NOT accepted as proof: its local reference compared a
     * legacy table->H2 transition with a generated table->paragraph transition.
     * That was an invalid apples-to-oranges geometry test. Live V103 then showed
     * no visible change, so its semantic adjacency selector cannot be treated as

     * actual following content node is a paragraph, a real 28px spacer element is
     * inserted. A real element cannot collapse with paragraph margins, unlike the
     * earlier margin-only fixes. Plain legacy Gutenberg tables stay untouched.
     */
    public static function enforce_generated_table_follow_gap_v150469() {
        if (is_admin() || !is_singular('post')) { return; }
        ?>
<style id="pftk-generated-table-follow-gap-v150469">
html body.single-post .entry-content .pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469{
  display:block!important;
  box-sizing:border-box!important;
  width:100%!important;
  height:28px!important;
  min-height:28px!important;
  margin:0!important;
  padding:0!important;
  border:0!important;
  background:transparent!important;
  clear:both!important;
  pointer-events:none!important;
}
</style>
<script id="pftk-generated-table-follow-gap-v150469-js">
(function(){
  var entry=document.querySelector('body.single-post .entry-content');
  if(!entry)return;

  function generatedTable(table){
    if(!table||table.tagName!=='TABLE')return false;
    if(table.matches('.system-129-table,.comparison-table'))return true;
    if(table.closest('article.ppm-generated,article[data-article-type]'))return true;
    if(table.closest('section[data-block="table"]'))return true;
    if(table.closest('.pm-table-wrap'))return true;
    if(table.matches('.comparison-table')&&table.querySelector('[data-fact-ids]'))return true;
    return false;
  }

  function outerBlock(table){
    var candidates=['.pm-table-wrap.wp-block-table','.comparison-table-wrap','figure.wp-block-table','.wp-block-table'];
    for(var i=0;i<candidates.length;i++){
      var n=table.closest(candidates[i]);
      if(n&&entry.contains(n))return n;
    }
    return table;
  }

  function nextElementAfter(node){
    var cur=node;
    while(cur&&cur!==entry){
      if(cur.nextElementSibling)return cur.nextElementSibling;
      cur=cur.parentElement;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function directFollowingParagraph(block){
    var next=nextElementAfter(block);
    if(!next)return null;
    if(next.tagName==='P')return next;
    // Nur transparente Einzelelement-Wrapper durchlaufen; niemals in den naechsten
    // semantischen Abschnitt hineinwandern. H2/H3 und Fazit bleiben unangetastet.
    if(next.matches('div:empty'))return null;
    if(next.matches('section,[data-block],h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6'))return null;
    var p=next.querySelector&&next.querySelector(':scope > p:first-child');
    return p||null;
  }

  function apply(){
    entry.querySelectorAll('table').forEach(function(table){
      if(!generatedTable(table))return;
      var block=outerBlock(table);
      var p=directFollowingParagraph(block);
      if(!p)return;
      block.style.setProperty('margin-bottom','0','important');
      var prev=p.previousElementSibling;
      if(prev&&prev.classList.contains('pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469'))return;
      var spacer=document.createElement('div');
      spacer.className='pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469';
      spacer.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
      p.parentNode.insertBefore(spacer,p);
    });
  }

  apply();
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',apply,{once:true});
  window.addEventListener('load',apply,{once:true});
  new MutationObserver(apply).observe(entry,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>
        <?php
    }

    public static function print_universal_content_axis_css() {
        if (is_admin() || is_feed() || is_front_page()) { return; }
        if (!is_singular(['page', 'post']) && !is_category() && !is_tax()) { return; }
        $layout = self::global_layout_settings();
        $axis = max(620, min(1400, intval($layout['content_axis_width'] ?? 900)));
        $post_width = max(620, min(1000, intval($layout['post_reading_width'] ?? 760)));
        $breadcrumb_width = max(620, min(1400, intval($layout['breadcrumb_axis_width'] ?? 900)));
        $breadcrumb_title_gap = max(0, min(80, intval($layout['breadcrumb_title_gap'] ?? 10)));
        $desktop = max(0, min(160, intval($layout['navigation_content_gap_desktop'] ?? 18)));
        $tablet = max(0, min(160, intval($layout['navigation_content_gap_tablet'] ?? 14)));
        $mobile = max(0, min(160, intval($layout['navigation_content_gap_mobile'] ?? 10)));
        $radius = max(0, min(40, intval($layout['global_card_radius'] ?? 12)));
        $title_image_gap = max(0, min(80, intval($layout['title_image_gap'] ?? 12)));
        echo '<style id="pftk-universal-content-axis-v15061">'
            . ':root{--pftk-content-axis-width:' . esc_attr($axis) . 'px;--pftk-post-reading-width:' . esc_attr($post_width) . 'px;--pftk-breadcrumb-axis-width:' . esc_attr($breadcrumb_width) . 'px;--pftk-navigation-content-gap:' . esc_attr($desktop) . 'px;--pftk-b\n\n========== EXACT HIT ==========\n\nPATTERN=entry-content\nOFFSET=1267840\nournal_chain : self::breadcrumb_portal_category_chain_v150310($term, true);
        }
        if (is_singular('post')) {
            $post_id = get_queried_object_id();
            $category = self::primary_article_category_v150297($post_id);
            if (is_object($category) && !empty($category->term_id)) {
                // Der aktuelle bindende Vertrag V4.99 endet beim echten Termnamen.
                // Der Beitragstitel bleibt bewusst ausserhalb des Breadcrumbs.
                return self::breadcrumb_portal_category_chain_v150310($category, false);
            }
        }
        return [];
    }

    private static function echo_breadcrumb_once($context) {
        static $rendered = [];
        if (!empty($rendered[$context]) || is_admin() || is_feed() || is_front_page()) { return; }
        $html = self::render_breadcrumb_markup(self::current_breadcrumb_crumbs(), true);
        if ($html === '') { return; }
        $rendered[$context] = true;
        echo '<div class="pftk-universal-breadcrumb-wrap pftk-breadcrumb-' . esc_attr($context) . '">' . $html . '</div>';
    }

    public static function render_singular_breadcrumb() {
        if (!is_singular(['page', 'post'])) { return; }
        self::echo_breadcrumb_once('singular');
    }

    public static function render_category_breadcrumb() {
        if (!is_category()) { return; }
        self::echo_breadcrumb_once('category');
    }


    public static function render_universal_breadcrumb_payload() {
        if (is_admin() || is_feed() || is_front_page()) { return; }
        if (!is_singular(['page', 'post']) && !is_category() && !is_tax()) { return; }
        $html = self::render_breadcrumb_markup(self::current_breadcrumb_crumbs(), true);
        if ($html === '') { return; }
        echo '<div id="pftk-breadcrumb-payload" hidden>' . $html . '</div>';
        echo <<<'HTML'
<script id="pftk-breadcrumb-mount-v150311">
(function(){
  function mount(){
    var payload=document.getElementById('pftk-breadcrumb-payload');
    var siteContent=document.querySelector('.site-content');
    if(!payload||!siteContent){return;}
    var nav=payload.querySelector('.pftk-content-breadcrumb');
    if(!nav){payload.remove();return;}
    var style=payload.querySelector('style');
    var schema=payload.querySelector('script[type="application/ld+json"]');
    if(style){document.head.appendChild(style);}
    if(schema){schema.id='pftk-breadcrumb-jsonld-v150311';document.head.appendChild(schema);}
    var mount=document.createElement('div');
    mount.className='pftk-universal-breadcrumb-wrap pftk-breadcrumb-mounted';
    mount.appendChild(nav);
    siteContent.insertBefore(mount,siteContent.firstChild);
    payload.remove();
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading'){document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',mount,{once:true});}
  else{mount();}
})();
</script>
HTML;
    }

        );
    }

    /**
     * V1.50.469 / Contract V104 – non-collapsing post-table gap for generated tables.
     *
     * V103 is explicitly NOT accepted as proof: its local reference compared a
     * legacy table->H2 transition with a generated table->paragraph transition.
     * That was an invalid apples-to-oranges geometry test. Live V103 then showed
     * no visible change, so its semantic adjacency selector cannot be treated as
     * a proven representation of the rendered WordPress DOM.
     *
     * V104 removes that dependency. The final browser DOM is inspected after the
     * article-design runtime has executed. Production-table signatures are the
     * table classes themselves (comparison-table/system-129-table), with the
     * semantic/article/wrapper markers retained as additional evidence. When the
     * actual following content node is a paragraph, a real 28px spacer element is
     * inserted. A real element cannot collapse with paragraph margins, unlike the
     * earlier margin-only fixes. Plain legacy Gutenberg tables stay untouched.
     */
    public static function enforce_generated_table_follow_gap_v150469() {
        if (is_admin() || !is_singular('post')) { return; }
        ?>
<style id="pftk-generated-table-follow-gap-v150469">
html body.single-post .entry-content .pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469{
  display:block!important;
  box-sizing:border-box!important;
  width:100%!important;
  height:28px!important;
  min-height:28px!important;
  margin:0!important;
  padding:0!important;
  border:0!important;
  background:transparent!important;
  clear:both!important;
  pointer-events:none!important;
}
</style>
<script id="pftk-generated-table-follow-gap-v150469-js">
(function(){
  var entry=document.querySelector('body.single-post .entry-content');
  if(!entry)return;

  function generatedTable(table){
    if(!table||table.tagName!=='TABLE')return false;
    if(table.matches('.system-129-table,.comparison-table'))return true;
    if(table.closest('article.ppm-generated,article[data-article-type]'))return true;
    if(table.closest('section[data-block="table"]'))return true;
    if(table.closest('.pm-table-wrap'))return true;
    if(table.matches('.comparison-table')&&table.querySelector('[data-fact-ids]'))return true;
    return false;
  }

  function outerBlock(table){
    var candidates=['.pm-table-wrap.wp-block-table','.comparison-table-wrap','figure.wp-block-table','.wp-block-table'];
    for(var i=0;i<candidates.length;i++){
      var n=table.closest(candidates[i]);
      if(n&&entry.contains(n))return n;
    }
    return table;
  }

  function nextElementAfter(node){
    var cur=node;
    while(cur&&cur!==entry){
      if(cur.nextElementSibling)return cur.nextElementSibling;
      cur=cur.parentElement;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function directFollowingParagraph(block){
    var next=nextElementAfter(block);
    if(!next)return null;
    if(next.tagName==='P')return next;
    // Nur transparente Einzelelement-Wrapper durchlaufen; niemals in den naechsten
    // semantischen Abschnitt hineinwandern. H2/H3 und Fazit bleiben unangetastet.
    if(next.matches('div:empty'))return null;
    if(next.matches('section,[data-block],h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6'))return null;
    var p=next.querySelector&&next.querySelector(':scope > p:first-child');
    return p||null;
  }

  function apply(){
    entry.querySelectorAll('table').forEach(function(table){
      if(!generatedTable(table))return;
      var block=outerBlock(table);
      var p=directFollowingParagraph(block);
      if(!p)return;
      block.style.setProperty('margin-bottom','0','important');
      var prev=p.previousElementSibling;
      if(prev&&prev.classList.contains('pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469'))return;
      var spacer=document.createElement('div');
      spacer.className='pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469';
      spacer.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
      p.parentNode.insertBefore(spacer,p);
    });
  }

  apply();
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',apply,{once:true});
  window.addEventListener('load',apply,{once:true});
  new MutationObserver(apply).observe(entry,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>
        <?php
    }

    public static function print_universal_content_axis_css() {
        if (is_admin() || is_feed() || is_front_page()) { return; }
        if (!is_singular(['page', 'post']) && !is_category() && !is_tax()) { return; }

    mount.appendChild(nav);
    siteContent.insertBefore(mount,siteContent.firstChild);
    payload.remove();
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading'){document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',mount,{once:true});}
  else{mount();}
})();
</script>
HTML;
    }

        $post_width = max(620, min(1000, intval($layout['post_reading_width'] ?? 760)));
        $breadcrumb_width = max(620, min(1400, intval($layout['breadcrumb_axis_width'] ?? 900)));
        $breadcrumb_title_gap = max(0, min(80, intval($layout['breadcrumb_title_gap'] ?? 10)));
        $desktop = max(0, min(160, intval($layout['navigation_content_gap_desktop'] ?? 18)));
        $tablet = max(0, min(160, intval($layout['navigation_content_gap_tablet'] ?? 14)));
        $mobile = max(0, min(160, intval($layout['navigation_content_gap_mobile'] ?? 10)));
        $radius = max(0, min(40, intval($layout['global_card_radius'] ?? 12)));
        $title_image_gap = max(0, min(80, intval($layout['title_image_gap'] ?? 12)));
        echo '<style id="pftk-universal-content-axis-v15061">'
            . ':root{--pftk-content-axis-width:' . esc_attr($axis) . 'px;--pftk-post-reading-width:' . esc_attr($post_width) . 'px;--pftk-breadcrumb-axis-width:' . esc_attr($breadcrumb_width) . 'px;--pftk-navigation-content-gap:' . esc_attr($desktop) . 'px;--pftk-breadcrumb-title-gap:' . esc_attr($breadcrumb_title_gap) . 'px;--pftk-global-card-radius:' . esc_attr($radius) . 'px;--pftk-title-image-gap:' . esc_attr($title_image_gap) . 'px}'
            . 'body.single-post .site-content,body.page .site-content,body.category .site-content,body.tax .site-content{padding-top:var(--pftk-navigation-content-gap)!important}'
            . 'body.single-post .ast-container,body.page .ast-container,body.category .ast-container,body.tax .ast-container{padding-top:0!important}'
            . '#pftk-breadcrumb-payload{display:none!important}'
            . '.pftk-universal-breadcrumb-wrap.pftk-breadcrumb-mounted{box-sizing:border-box!important;position:relative!important;width:min(calc(100vw - 32px),var(--pftk-breadcrumb-axis-width))!important;max-width:none!important;margin:0 auto var(--pftk-breadcrumb-title-gap)!important;transform:none!important;padding:0!important;clear:both!important}'
            . '.pftk-universal-breadcrumb-wrap.pftk-breadcrumb-mounted .pftk-content-breadcrumb{margin:0!important}'
            . 'body.single-post .site-content>.pftk-breadcrumb-mounted+ .ast-container,body.page .site-content>.pftk-breadcrumb-mounted+ .ast-container,body.category .site-content>.pftk-breadcrumb-mounted+ .ast-container,body.tax .site-content>.pftk-breadcrumb-mounted+ .ast-container{margin-top:0!important;padding-top:0!important}'
            .     /**
     * V1.50.469 / Contract V104 – non-collapsing post-table gap for generated tables.
     *
     * V103 is explicitly NOT accepted as proof: its local reference compared a
     * legacy table->H2 transition with a generated table->paragraph transition.
     * That was an invalid apples-to-oranges geometry test. Live V103 then showed
     * no visible change, so its semantic adjacency selector cannot be treated as
     * a proven representation of the rendered WordPress DOM.
     *
     * V104 removes that dependency. The final browser DOM is inspected after the
     * article-design runtime has executed. Production-table signatures are the
     * table classes themselves (comparison-table/system-129-table), with the
     * semantic/article/wrapper markers retained as additional evidence. When the
     * actual following content node is a paragraph, a real 28px spacer element is
     * inserted. A real element cannot collapse with paragraph margins, unlike the
     * earlier margin-only fixes. Plain legacy Gutenberg tables stay untouched.
     */
    public static function enforce_generated_table_follow_gap_v150469() {
        if (is_admin() || !is_singular('post')) { return; }
        ?>
<style id="pftk-generated-table-follow-gap-v150469">
html body.single-post .entry-content .pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469{
  display:block!important;
  box-sizing:border-box!important;
  width:100%!important;
  height:28px!important;
  min-height:28px!important;
  margin:0!important;
  padding:0!important;
  border:0!important;
  background:transparent!important;
  clear:both!important;
  pointer-events:none!important;
}
</style>
<script id="pftk-generated-table-follow-gap-v150469-js">
(function(){
  var entry=document.querySelector('body.single-post .entry-content');
  if(!entry)return;

  function generatedTable(table){
    if(!table||table.tagName!=='TABLE')return false;
    if(table.matches('.system-129-table,.comparison-table'))return true;
    if(table.closest('article.ppm-generated,article[data-article-type]'))return true;
    if(table.closest('section[data-block="table"]'))return true;
    if(table.closest('.pm-table-wrap'))return true;
    if(table.matches('.comparison-table')&&table.querySelector('[data-fact-ids]'))return true;
    return false;
  }

  function outerBlock(table){
    var candidates=['.pm-table-wrap.wp-block-table','.comparison-table-wrap','figure.wp-block-table','.wp-block-table'];
    for(var i=0;i<candidates.length;i++){
      var n=table.closest(candidates[i]);
      if(n&&entry.contains(n))return n;
    }
    return table;
  }

  function nextElementAfter(node){
    var cur=node;
    while(cur&&cur!==entry){
      if(cur.nextElementSibling)return cur.nextElementSibling;
      cur=cur.parentElement;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function directFollowingParagraph(block){
    var next=nextElementAfter(block);
    if(!next)return null;
    if(next.tagName==='P')return next;
    // Nur transparente Einzelelement-Wrapper durchlaufen; niemals in den naechsten
    // semantischen Abschnitt hineinwandern. H2/H3 und Fazit bleiben unangetastet.
    if(next.matches('div:empty'))return null;
    if(next.matches('section,[data-block],h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6'))return null;
    var p=next.querySelector&&next.querySelector(':scope > p:first-child');
    return p||null;
  }

  function apply(){
    entry.querySelectorAll('table').forEach(function(table){
      if(!generatedTable(table))return;
      var block=outerBlock(table);
      var p=directFollowingParagraph(block);
      if(!p)return;
      block.style.setProperty('margin-bottom','0','important');
      var prev=p.previousElementSibling;
      if(prev&&prev.classList.contains('pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469'))return;
      var spacer=document.createElement('div');
      spacer.className='pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469';
      spacer.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
      p.parentNode.insertBefore(spacer,p);
    });
  }

  apply();
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',apply,{once:true});
  window.addEventListener('load',apply,{once:true});
  new MutationObserver(apply).observe(entry,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>
        <?php
    }

    var p=next.querySelector&&next.querySelector(':scope > p:first-child');
    return p||null;
  }

  function apply(){
    entry.querySelectorAll('table').forEach(function(table){
      if(!generatedTable(table))return;
      var block=outerBlock(table);
      var p=directFollowingParagraph(block);
      if(!p)return;
      block.style.setProperty('margin-bottom','0','important');
      var prev=p.previousElementSibling;
      if(prev&&prev.classList.contains('pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469'))return;
      var spacer=document.createElement('div');
      spacer.className='pa-ppm-table-follow-spacer-v150469';
      spacer.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
      p.parentNode.insertBefore(spacer,p);
    });
  }

  apply();
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',apply,{once:true});
  window.addEventListener('load',apply,{once:true});
  new MutationObserver(apply).observe(entry,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

  var queued=false;
  function queue(){if(queued)return;queued=true;requestAnimationFrame(function(){queued=false;scan();});}

    public static function placeholder_image_autocrop_script() {
        if (is_admin()) { return; }
        echo <<<'HTML'
<script id="pftk-placeholder-autocrop-v15036">
(function(){
  var selector=".pftk .pftk-card.pftk-card-ad[data-pftk-placeholder=\"1\"]";
  var cache={};
  function state(card,value){card.setAttribute("data-pftk-autocrop",value);}
  function createCrop(src){
    if(cache[src])return cache[src];
    cache[src]=new Promise(function(resolve){
      var image=new Image();
      image.decoding="async";
      image.onload=function(){
        try{
          var w=image.naturalWidth||0,h=image.naturalHeight||0;
          if(w<2||h<2){resolve("");return;}
          var scale=Math.min(1,800/Math.max(w,h));
          var sw=Math.max(1,Math.round(w*scale)),sh=Math.max(1,Math.round(h*scale));
          var scan=document.createElement("canvas");scan.width=sw;scan.height=sh;
          var ctx=scan.getContext("2d",{willReadFrequently:true});
          ctx.drawImage(image,0,0,sw,sh);
          var pixels=ctx.getImageData(0,0,sw,sh).data;
          function pale(x,y){var i=(y*sw+x)*4;return pixels[i+3]<16||(pixels[i]>244&&pixels[i+1]>244&&pixels[i+2]>244);}
          var inset=Math.max(1,Math.round(Math.min(sw,sh)*0.01));
          var corners=[[inset,inset],[sw-1-inset,inset],[inset,sh-1-inset],[sw-1-inset,sh-1-inset]];
          var paleCorners=0;for(var c=0;c<corners.length;c++){if(pale(corners[c][0],corners[c][1]))paleCorners++;}
          if(paleCorners<3){resolve("");return;}
          var minX=sw,minY=sh,maxX=-1,maxY=-1;
          var step=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Math.max(sw,sh)/600));
          for(var y=0;y<sh;y+=step){for(var x=0;x<sw;x+=step){if(!pale(x,y)){if(x<minX)minX=x;if(x>maxX)maxX=x;if(y<minY)minY=y;if(y>maxY)maxY=y;}}}
          if(maxX<minX||maxY<minY){resolve("");return;}
          var boxW=maxX-minX+1,boxH=maxY-minY+1;
          if(boxW/sw>0.94&&boxH/sh>0.94){resolve("");return;}
          // Kein Aussen-Padding: Genau dieses Padding erzeugte live die verbleibenden weissen Seitenstreifen.
          // Horizontal wird 1 % innerhalb der erkannten Motivkante geschnitten; vertikal bleibt die echte
          // Motivkante erhalten, weil `cover` dort ohnehin passend beschneidet.
          var trimX=Math.max(2,Math.round(boxW*0.01));
          minX=Math.min(maxX-1,minX+trimX);maxX=Math.max(minX+1,maxX-trimX);
          var sx=Math.max(0,Math.floor(minX/scale)),sy=Math.max(0,Math.floor(minY/scale));
          var cw=Math.min(w-sx,Math.ceil((maxX-minX+1)/scale)),ch=Math.min(h-sy,Math.ceil((maxY-minY+1)/scale));
          if(cw<2||ch<2){resolve("");return;}
          var out=document.createElement("canvas");out.width=cw;out.height=ch;
          out.getContext("2d").drawImage(image,sx,sy,cw,ch,0,0,cw,ch);
          out.toBlob(function(blob){resolve(blob?URL.createObjectURL(blob):"");},"image/webp",0.92);
        }catch(error){resolve("");}
      };
      image.onerror=function(){resolve("");};
      image.src=src;
    });
    return cache[src];
  }
  function apply(card){
    if(card.getAttribute("data-pftk-autocrop"))return;
    state(card,"pending");
    var img=card.querySelector(".pftk-hub-grid-ad-image>img");
    var src=img?(img.currentSrc||img.src||""):"";
    if(!src){state(card,"no-image");return;}
    createCrop(src).then(function(url){if(url){card.style.setProperty("background-image","url(\""+url+"\")","important");state(card,"done");}else{state(card,"skipped");}});
  }
  function run(){document.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(apply);}
  if(document.readyState==="loading")document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",run);else run();
  window.addEventListener("load",run,{once:true});
  new MutationObserver(run).observe(document.documentElement,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>
HTML;
    }

    public static function article_design_script() {
        if (is_admin() || !is_singular('post')) { return; }
        echo <<<'HTML'
<script id="pftk-article-design-v150365">
(function(){
  var c=document.querySelector("body.single-post .entry-content");
  if(!c)return;

  var norm=function(v){
    return String(v||"").replace(/\u00a0/g," ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim().toLowerCase();
  };
  var isMedia=function(e){
    return !!(e&&(
      (e.matches&&e.matches("figure,.wp-block-image,.wp-caption,picture"))||
      (e.querySelector&&e.querySelector("img"))
    ));
  };
  var firstTextParagraph=function(root){
    if(!root)return null;
    return Array.prototype.slice.call(root.querySelectorAll("p")).find(function(p){
      return norm(p.textContent).length>0;
    })||null;
  };
  var topChildOf=function(root,node){
    var current=node;
    while(current&&current.parentElement&&current.parentElement!==root){
      current=current.parentElement;
    }
    return current&&current.parentElement===root?current:null;
  };
  var findExternalFeaturedImage=function(){
    var themeArticle=c.closest("article");
    if(!themeArticle)return null;
    var candidate=themeArticle.querySelector(
      ".ast-single-post-featured-section,.post-thumb,.post-thumb-img-content,.ast-blog-featured-section"
    );
    if(!candidate||c.contains(candidate))return null;
    var outer=candidate;
    while(outer.parentElement&&outer.parentElement!==themeArticle&&outer.parentElement!==c){
      outer=outer.parentElement;
    }
    return outer;
  };
  var findAdjacentMediaBefore=function(root,node){
    var current=node;
    while(current&&current!==root){
      var previous=current.previousElementSibling;
      while(previous&&
        !isMedia(previous)&&
        !norm(previous.textContent).length&&
        !previous.querySelector("h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,p,ul,ol,table,blockquote")){
        previous=previous.previousElementSibling;
      }
      if(previous&&isMedia(previous))return previous;
      current=current.parentElement;
    }
    return null;
  };

  /* V1.50.365: Produktionsmaschinen-Artikel werden nicht nur an der
     optionalen Klasse, sondern auch am verbindlichen data-article-type
     erkannt. Ohne diese Erkennung wurde ein inneres article-Element ohne
     ppm-generated fälschlich als kompletter Introinhalt behandelt und damit
     der gesamte Beitrag farbig hinterlegt. */
  var structured=c.querySelector("article.ppm-generated,article[data-article-type]");
  if(structured&&!structured.classList.contains("ppm-generated")){
    structured.classList.add("ppm-generated");
  }
  var scope=structured||c;
  var intro=null;
  var firstP=null;
  var photo=null;

  if(structured){
    structured.classList.remove("pa-ppm-compat-v150290");
    structured.classList.add("pa-ppm-compat-v150291");

    var structuredDirect=Array.prototype.slice.call(structured.children);
    var firstStructuredHeading=Array.prototype.slice.call(structured.querySelectorAll("h2,h3")).find(function(h){
      return !h.closest('section[data-block="conclusion"]');
    })||null;
    var headingTop=topChildOf(structured,firstStructuredHeading);
    var structuredBoundary=headingTop?structuredDirect.indexOf(headingTop):structuredDirect.length;
    if(structuredBoundary<0)structuredBoundary=structuredDirect.length;
    var structuredBefore=structuredDirect.slice(0,structuredBoundary).filter(function(e){
      return !/^(SCRIPT|STYLE|NOSCRIPT)$/.test(e.tagName)&&
        !e.matches('section[data-block="conclusion"]');
    });
    var isBeforeBoundary=function(node,boundaryNode){
      if(!node)return false;
      if(!boundaryNode)return true;
      return !!(node.compareDocumentPosition(boundaryNode)&Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING);
    };
    var introParagraphs=Array.prototype.slice.call(structured.querySelectorAll("p")).filter(function(p){
      if(!norm(p.textContent).length)return false;
      if(p.closest("figure,figcaption,table,thead,tbody,tfoot,ul,ol,blockquote"))return false;
      if(p.closest('section[data-block="conclusion"]'))return false;
      return isBeforeBoundary(p,firstStructuredHeading);
    }).slice(0,2);

    intro=structured.querySelector('section[data-block="intro"]');
    photo=structuredBefore.find(function(e){
      return e!==intro&&e.tagName!=="H1"&&isMedia(e);
    })||null;

    /* V1.50.292: Ein nachträglich im WordPress-Editor eingefügtes Bild kann
       unmittelbar VOR dem inneren article.ppm-generated liegen. V1.50.291
       suchte nur innerhalb des strukturierten Artikels und konnte diesen
       realen DOM-Fall deshalb nicht rahmen. Es wird ausschließlich das direkt
       angrenzende Medienelement vor dem strukturierten Artikel bzw. dessen
       äußerem Wrapper übernommen. */
    if(!photo){
      photo=findAdjacentMediaBefore(c,structured);
    }

    if(!photo&&intro){
      var embedded=intro.querySelector("figure,.wp-block-image,.wp-caption,picture,img");
      if(embedded){
        photo=embedded.closest("figure,.wp-block-image,.wp-caption,picture")||embedded;
      }
    }

    if(!photo){
      photo=findExternalFeaturedImage();
    }

    /* V1.50.291: Der obere Text kann bei Produktionsmaschinen-Beiträgen
       direkt oder in einem gemeinsamen Inhaltswrapper liegen. Unabhängig von
       dieser Verschachtelung werden die ersten beiden echten Absätze vor der
       ersten H2/H3 als Intro erfasst. */
    if(!intro&&introParagraphs.length){
      intro=document.createElement("section");
      intro.className="pa-article-intro pa-ppm-reconstructed-intro-v150291";
      introParagraphs[0].parentNode.insertBefore(intro,introParagraphs[0]);
    }
    if(intro&&introParagraphs.length){
      introParagraphs.forEach(function(p){
        if(!intro.contains(p))intro.appendChild(p);
      });
    }

    if(intro){
      intro.classList.add("pa-article-intro");
      firstP=firstTextParagraph(intro);
      if(firstP)firstP.classList.add("pa-article-intro-first");
    }

    /* V1.50.291: Vorhandene Teilrahmen werden nicht übersprungen, sondern
       normalisiert. Dadurch liegen Bild UND die beiden ersten echten Absätze
       nachweislich im selben Rahmen – auch wenn article.ppm-generated in einem
       zusätzlichen WordPress-/Block-Wrapper verschachtelt ist. */
    var frame=structured.querySelector(":scope > .pa-photo-intro-frame")||
      c.querySelector(":scope > .pa-photo-intro-frame");

    if(intro&&photo){
      if(frame&&photo===frame){
        photo=frame.querySelector(":scope > .pa-photo-intro-media")||
          frame.querySelector("figure,.wp-block-image,.wp-caption,picture,img");
      }
      if(!photo)return;
      photo.classList.add("pa-photo-intro-media");

      if(!frame){
        frame=document.createElement("section"\n\n========== EXACT HIT ==========\n\nPATTERN=ppm-generated\nOFFSET=1270548\nremove();
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading'){document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',mount,{once:true});}
  else{mount();}
})();
</script>
HTML;
    }

    public static function enqueue_single_post_locked_style() {
